<file path="design-system-canvas-leafletmap.txt">
# Design System/canvas/LeafletMap

Leaflet map wrapper. Re-exports all `react-leaflet` named exports (`Marker`, `Popup`, `TileLayer`, etc.) from `@metris/ui/canvas/map`. Consumers must import `leaflet/dist/leaflet.css` in their app.

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `onMapClick` | `((e: { lat: number; lng: number; }) => void)` | No |  | Callback fired when the user clicks on the map. |
| `height` | `string` | No | 400px | CSS height of the map container. |


## Examples


### With Pre Placed Markers

Map with pre-placed markers

```tsx
{
  render: () => {
    const markers: Array<{
      position: [number, number];
      label: string;
    }> = [{
      position: [48.2082, 16.3738],
      label: 'Vienna'
    }, {
      position: [48.1351, 11.582],
      label: 'Munich'
    }, {
      position: [47.3769, 8.5417],
      label: 'Zurich'
    }];
    return <LeafletMap center={[47.8, 13.0]} zoom={6} height="400px">
        {markers.map(({
        position,
        label
      }) => <Marker key={label} position={position}>
            <Popup>{label}</Popup>
          </Marker>)}
      </LeafletMap>;
  }
}
```

### With Click To Place Marker

Click anywhere on the map to place a marker

```tsx
{
  render: () => <ClickToPlaceMarkerDemo />
}
```

### Playground

Default map centered on Vienna, Austria

```tsx
{
  args: {
    center: [48.2082, 16.3738],
    zoom: 12,
    height: '400px'
  }
}
```
</file>

<file path="design-system-canvas-reactflow.txt">
# Design System/canvas/ReactFlow


ReactFlow is a powerful and flexible flow diagram component built on top of @xyflow/react.

## Features

- **Multiple Node Types**: Support for different node appearances (quadHandler, doubleHandler, noHandler)
- **Custom Edge Types**: Dynamic edge routing with floating edge support
- **Context Menu Integration**: Right-click context menus for nodes, edges, and canvas
- **Appearance Mapping**: Map node/edge types to visual variants dynamically
- **Monitor Mode**: Display real-time monitored values on nodes with status indicators
- **Interactive**: Full support for dragging, connecting, and editing flow elements
- **Customizable**: Extensible with custom node/edge components and styling

## Node Types

- `quadHandler`: Four connection handles (top, bottom, left, right) - default
- `doubleHandler`: Two connection handles (top, bottom) - for linear flows
- `noHandler`: No visible handles - for display-only nodes

## Edge Types

- `floating`: Smart edge routing that adapts to node positions and types

## Monitor Mode

Enable `monitorMode` to display real-time values on nodes:
- Set `monitorMode={true}` on the ReactFlow component
- Add `monitors` array to node data with value, unit, and status for each monitor
- Values appear as badges at the top-right corner of nodes, expanding to the right
- Status colors: normal (green), warning (orange), critical (red)
- Supports multiple monitors per node for displaying different values/units

## Usage

The component accepts type-safe mappings for node/edge types, appearances, and context menus.
Use the stories below to explore different configurations and interactions.
        

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `nodeContexts` | `Record<NodeMap, ContextMenuItem<Node>[]>` | No | {} as Record<NodeMap, ContextMenuItem<Node>[]> | Context menu items mapped by node type identifier |
| `nodeAppearance` | `Record<NodeMap, NodeAppearanceType>` | No |  | Appearance mapping for each node type to visual variant |
| `edgeContexts` | `Record<EdgeMap, ContextMenuItem<Edge>[]>` | No | {} as Record<EdgeMap, ContextMenuItem<Edge>[]> | Context menu items mapped by edge type identifier |
| `edgeAppearance` | `Record<EdgeMap, EdgeAppearanceType>` | No |  | Appearance mapping for each edge type to visual variant |
| `canvasContext` | `ContextMenuItem<null>[]` | No | [] | Context menu items for the canvas background (when no node/edge is selected) |
| `defaultNodeAppearance` | `string` | No |  | Default appearance for nodes without a specific mapping |
| `defaultEdgeAppearance` | `string` | No |  | Default appearance for edges without a specific mapping |
| `monitorMode` | `boolean` | No | false | Enable monitoring mode to display monitored values on nodes |


## Examples


### Mapped Appearance

Demonstrates node type to appearance mapping

Shows how different node types ('quad', 'double', 'none') are mapped to visual appearances
(quadHandler, doubleHandler, noHandler) and includes an unmapped node using default appearance.

```tsx
{
  render: args => InteractiveFlow(args),
  args: {
    nodeAppearance: {
      quad: 'quadHandler',
      double: 'doubleHandler',
      none: 'noHandler'
    },
    nodes: [{
      id: '1',
      position: {
        x: 400,
        y: 400
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Quad',
        nodeType: 'quad'
      }
    }, {
      id: '2',
      position: {
        x: 575,
        y: 400
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Double',
        nodeType: 'double'
      }
    }, {
      id: '3',
      position: {
        x: 450,
        y: 450
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Quad',
        nodeType: 'quad'
      }
    }, {
      id: '4',
      data: {
        label: 'Double',
        nodeType: 'double'
      },
      position: {
        x: 300,
        y: 500
      }
    }, {
      id: '5',
      data: {
        label: 'Quad',
        nodeType: 'quad'
      },
      position: {
        x: 350,
        y: 550
      }
    }, {
      id: '6',
      data: {
        label: 'Double',
        nodeType: 'double'
      },
      position: {
        x: 500,
        y: 550
      }
    }, {
      id: '7',
      data: {
        label: 'None',
        nodeType: 'none',
        hiddenPoint: true
      },
      position: {
        x: 600,
        y: 600
      }
    }, {
      id: '8',
      data: {
        label: 'No type'
      },
      position: {
        x: 700,
        y: 700
      }
    }],
    edges: [{
      id: 'e1-2',
      source: '1',
      target: '2'
    }, {
      id: 'e2-3',
      source: '2',
      target: '3'
    }, {
      id: 'e3-4',
      source: '3',
      target: '4'
    }, {
      id: 'e4-5',
      source: '4',
      target: '5'
    }, {
      id: 'e5-6',
      source: '5',
      target: '6'
    }, {
      id: 'e6-7',
      source: '6',
      target: '7'
    }]
  }
}
```

### Monitor Mode

Demonstrates Monitor Mode for displaying real-time values on nodes

When monitorMode is enabled, nodes can display monitored values at the top-right corner
with color-coded status indicators (normal=green, warning=orange, critical=red).
Perfect for dashboards, real-time monitoring, and process control visualizations.

```tsx
{
  render: args => InteractiveFlow(args),
  args: {
    ...storyMappings,
    monitorMode: true,
    nodes: [{
      id: '1',
      position: {
        x: 100,
        y: 100
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Temp Sensor',
        nodeType: 'quad',
        size: 'large',
        monitors: [{
          value: 72.5,
          unit: '°C',
          status: 'normal'
        }, {
          value: 162.5,
          unit: '°F',
          status: 'normal'
        }]
      }
    }, {
      id: '2',
      position: {
        x: 320,
        y: 100
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Pressure',
        nodeType: 'quad',
        size: 'large',
        monitors: [{
          name: 'Tag1',
          value: 145,
          unit: 'PSI',
          status: 'warning'
        }, {
          value: 10,
          unit: 'bar',
          status: 'warning'
        }]
      }
    }, {
      id: '3',
      position: {
        x: 540,
        y: 100
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Flow Rate',
        nodeType: 'quad',
        size: 'large',
        monitors: [{
          value: 98,
          unit: '%',
          status: 'critical'
        }, {
          value: 245,
          unit: 'L/min',
          status: 'critical'
        }]
      }
    }, {
      id: '4',
      position: {
        x: 100,
        y: 200
      },
      data: {
        label: 'RPM',
        nodeType: 'double',
        size: 'small',
        monitors: [{
          value: 1850,
          unit: 'RPM',
          status: 'normal'
        }, {
          value: 'ON',
          status: 'normal'
        }]
      }
    }, {
      id: '5',
      position: {
        x: 320,
        y: 200
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Voltage',
        nodeType: 'double',
        size: 'small',
        monitors: [{
          value: 24.8,
          unit: 'V',
          status: 'normal'
        }]
      }
    }, {
      id: '6',
      position: {
        x: 540,
        y: 200
      },
      data: {
        label: 'No Monitor',
        nodeType: 'none',
        size: 'large'
        // No monitor data - this node won't show a monitor badge
      }
    }],
    edges: [{
      id: 'e1-4',
      source: '1',
      target: '4'
    }, {
      id: 'e2-5',
      source: '2',
      target: '5'
    }, {
      id: 'e3-6',
      source: '3',
      target: '6'
    }]
  }
}
```

### Menu Context Usage

Demonstrates context menu integration for canvas and different node types

Shows canvas context menus, node-specific context menus for each type ('quad', 'double', 'none'),
and fallback behavior for unmapped node types.

```tsx
{
  render: args => InteractiveFlow(args),
  args: {
    ...storyMappings,
    canvasContext: [{
      label: 'Add Hub Node',
      icon: <AbcOutlinedIcon />,
      action: fn()
    }, {
      label: 'Add Process Node',
      icon: <AbcOutlinedIcon />,
      action: fn()
    }, {
      label: 'Add Display Node',
      icon: <AbcOutlinedIcon />,
      action: fn()
    }, {
      label: 'Auto Layout',
      icon: <AbcOutlinedIcon />,
      action: fn()
    }],
    nodes: [{
      id: '1',
      position: {
        x: 200,
        y: 100
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Input Hub',
        nodeType: 'quad'
      }
    }, {
      id: '2',
      position: {
        x: 400,
        y: 100
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Processor',
        nodeType: 'double'
      }
    }, {
      id: '3',
      position: {
        x: 600,
        y: 100
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Output Display',
        nodeType: 'none'
      }
    }, {
      id: '4',
      position: {
        x: 200,
        y: 250
      },
      data: {
        label: 'Unmapped Type'
      } // Will use defaultAppearance
    }],
    edges: [{
      id: 'e1-2',
      source: '1',
      target: '2'
    }, {
      id: 'e2-3',
      source: '2',
      target: '3'
    }, {
      id: 'e1-4',
      source: '1',
      target: '4'
```

### Hidden Point Behavior

# Design System/charts/BarChart

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `data` | `BarChartDataItem[]` | Yes |  |  |
| `yLabel` | `string` | No | Value |  |
| `width` | `number` | No |  |  |
| `height` | `number` | No | 300 |  |
| `color` | `string` | No | #40A6FF |  |
| `categoryGapRatio` | `number` | No | 0.6 |  |
| `loading` | `boolean` | No | false |  |
| `showGrid` | `boolean` | No | true |  |


## Examples


### Loading

```tsx
{
  args: {
    data: [],
    yLabel: 'Value',
    loading: true
  }
}
```

### Fridge Example

```tsx
{
  args: {
    data: [{
      label: 'Category A',
      value: 120
    }, {
      label: 'Category B',
      value: 89
    }, {
      label: 'Category C',
      value: 156
    }, {
      label: 'Category D',
      value: 78
    }, {
      label: 'Category E',
      value: 143
    }],
    yLabel: 'Count',
    color: '#40A6FF',
    loading: false
  }
}
```

### Custom Color

```tsx
{
  args: {
    data: [{
      label: 'Product 1',
      value: 230
    }, {
      label: 'Product 2',
      value: 180
    }, {
      label: 'Product 3',
      value: 310
    }, {
      label: 'Product 4',
      value: 145
    }],
    yLabel: 'Revenue',
    color: '#FF6B6B',
    loading: false
  }
}
```

### Without Grid

```tsx
{

    data: [{
      label: 'Q1',

    }, {
      label: 'Q2',

    }, {
      label: 'Q3',
      value: 280
    }, {
      label: 'Q4',
      value: 350
    }],
    yLabel: 'Quarterly Sales',
    showGrid: false,
    loading: false
  }
}
```

### Default

```tsx
  }
}
```

<file path="design-system-charts-chartcard-interactionspanel.txt">
# Design System/charts/ChartCard/InteractionsPanel

InteractionsPanel exposes zoom/pan, rollover, and mouse wheel interaction configuration grouped in tabs. State and persistence are fully external.

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `title` | `string` | No | Interactions |  |
| `width` | `string | number` | No | 420 |  |
| `sx` | `SxProps` | No |  |  |
| `values` | `InteractionValues` | Yes |  |  |
| `editing` | `InteractionsEditingState<T>` | Yes |  |  |
| `zoomOnOptions` | `InteractionOption[]` | Yes |  |  |
| `panOnOptions` | `InteractionOption[]` | Yes |  |  |
| `panningDirectionOptions` | `InteractionOption[]` | Yes |  |  |
| `showTooltipOnOptions` | `InteractionOption[]` | Yes |  |  |
| `rolloverTooltipOnOptions` | `InteractionOption[]` | Yes |  |  |
| `mouseWheelActionOptions` | `InteractionOption[]` | Yes |  |  |
| `mouseWheelDirectionOptions` | `InteractionOption[]` | Yes |  |  |
| `activeTab` | `number` | No |  |  |
| `onTabChange` | `((tab: number) => void)` | No |  |  |
| `defaultTab` | `number` | No | 0 |  |
| `onChangeValues` | `(patch: Partial<InteractionValues>) => void` | Yes |  |  |
| `onChangeLayoutDefinition` | `(layoutDefinition: T) => void` | Yes |  |  |
| `onSave` | `() => void` | Yes |  |  |
| `onCancel` | `() => void` | Yes |  |  |
| `onClose` | `() => void` | Yes |  |  |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | No | false |  |
| `saveDisabled` | `boolean` | No |  |  |
| `cancelDisabled` | `boolean` | No |  |  |
| `labels` | `(Omit<Partial<InteractionsPanelTextLabels>, "tabs" | "sections" | "toggles" | "fields"> & { tabs?: Partial<{ zoomPan: string; rollover: string; mouseWheel: string; }>; sections?: Partial<...>; toggles?: Partial<...> | undefined; fields?: Partial<...> | undefined; }) | undefined` | No |  |  |


## Examples


### Default

```tsx
{
  render: () => {
    const values: InteractionValues = {
      zoomXAxisOnly: false,
      executeZoomOn: zoomOnOptions[0].value as number,
      executePanOn: panOnOptions[0].value as number,
      panningDirection: panningDirectionOptions[0].value as number,
      rolloverEnabled: true,
      rolloverShowDescription: true,
      rolloverShowName: true,
      rolloverTooltipOn: rolloverTooltipOnOptions[0].value as number,
      showTooltipOn: showTooltipOnOptions[0].value as number,
      mouseWheelEnabled: true,
      mouseWheelAction: mouseWheelActionOptions[0].value as number,
      mouseWheelDirection: mouseWheelDirectionOptions[0].value as number
    };
    const editing: InteractionsEditingState = {
      layoutDefinition: layoutDefinitions[0].value,
      layoutDefinitions,
      isSaving: false,
      saveError: null,
      saveSuccess: false,
      hasUnsavedChanges: false
    };
    return <InteractionsPanel values={values} editing={editing} zoomOnOptions={zoomOnOptions} panOnOptions={panOnOptions} panningDirectionOptions={panningDirectionOptions} showTooltipOnOptions={showTooltipOnOptions} rolloverTooltipOnOptions={rolloverTooltipOnOptions} mouseWheelActionOptions={mouseWheelActionOptions} mouseWheelDirectionOptions={mouseWheelDirectionOptions} onChangeValues={p => console.log('Interaction change', p)} onChangeLayoutDefinition={def => console.log('Layout def change', def)} onSave={() => console.log('Save clicked')} onCancel={() => console.log('Cancel clicked')} onClose={() => console.log('Close panel')} />;
  }
}
```

# Design System/charts/ChartCard/LayoutPanel

LayoutPanel displays chart layout configuration controls (graphic & legend) with externally managed state.

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `graphic` | `LayoutGraphicValues` | Yes |  |  |
| `editing` | `LayoutEditingState<T>` | Yes |  |  |
| `placementOptions` | `{ value: number; text: string; }[]` | No |  | Legend placement options. When omitted the Placement select is hidden. |
| `sourceModeOptions` | `{ value: number; text: string; }[]` | No |  | Data source mode options. When omitted the "Data for" select is hidden. |
| `themeOptions` | `{ value: string | number; text: string; }[]` | Yes |  |  |
| `onChangeGraphic` | `(patch: Partial<LayoutGraphicValues>) => void` | Yes |  |  |
| `onChangeLayoutDefinition` | `(def: T) => void` | Yes |  |  |
| `onSave` | `(() => void)` | No |  | Save callback. When omitted the Save button is hidden. |
| `onCancel` | `(() => void)` | No |  | Cancel callback. When omitted the Cancel button is hidden. |
| `onClose` | `() => void` | Yes |  |  |
| `title` | `string` | No | LAYOUT |  |
| `disabled` | `boolean` | No | false |  |
| `activeTab` | `number` | No |  | Optional controlled active tab index (0: Graphic, 1: Legend). If omitted component is uncontrolled. |
| `defaultTab` | `number` | No | 0 | Initial tab for uncontrolled usage. |
| `onTabChange` | `((tabIndex: number) => void)` | No |  | Tab change callback (fires for both controlled/uncontrolled). |
| `saveDisabled` | `boolean` | No |  | Override save button disabled state. |
| `cancelDisabled` | `boolean` | No |  | Override cancel button disabled state. |
| `labels` | `Partial<LayoutPanelTextLabels>` | No |  | Override labels & i18n hooks |
| `togglesConfig` | `ToggleConfig[]` | No |  | Optional dynamic toggle configuration; if omitted falls back to legacy hard-coded switches |
| `width` | `string | number` | No | 320 | Custom width (defaults to 320) |
| `sx` | `SxProps` | No |  | Additional sx overrides merged last. Use system SxProps shape if exposed in consuming UI layer. |


## Examples


### Default

```tsx
{
  render: () => {
    const graphic: LayoutGraphicValues = {
      showAllYAxis: false,
      showAllLines: true,
      showPointMarkers: false,
      showTodayLine: false,
      showGaps: false,
      showLegend: true,
      showLegendCheckboxes: true,
      showSeriesMarkers: true,
      legendPlacement: 3,
      legendSourceMode: sourceModeOptions[0].value,
      trendTheme: themeOptions[0].text
    };
    const editing: LayoutEditingState = {
      layoutDefinition: layoutDefinitions[0].value,
      layoutDefinitions,
      isBandedAxis: false,
      loadingPreferences: false,
      preferencesError: null,
      isSaving: false,
      saveError: null,
      saveSuccess: false,
      hasUnsavedChanges: false
    };
    return <LayoutPanel graphic={graphic} editing={editing} placementOptions={placementOptions} sourceModeOptions={sourceModeOptions} themeOptions={themeOptions} onChangeGraphic={p => console.log('Graphic change', p)} onChangeLayoutDefinition={def => console.log('Layout definition change', def)} onSave={() => console.log('Save clicked')} onCancel={() => console.log('Cancel clicked')} onClose={() => console.log('LayoutPanel closed')} labels={{
      layoutSource: 'Layout Source',
      tabs: {
        graphic: 'Graphic',
        legend: 'Legend'
      },
      save: 'Save'
    }} togglesConfig={[{
      key: 'showAllLines'
    }, {
      key: 'showPointMarkers'
    }, {
      key: 'showTodayLine'
    }, {
      key: 'showGaps'
    }, {
      key: 'showLegend'
    }, {
      key: 'showLegendCheckboxes',
      dependsOn: 'showLegend'
    }, {
      key: 'showSeriesMarkers',
      dependsOn: 'showLegend'
    }]} />;
  }
}
```

```tsx
      layoutDefinitions: [],

      loadingPreferences: false,
      preferencesError: null,
      isSaving: false,
      saveError: null,
      saveSuccess: false,
      hasUnsavedChanges: false
    };
    return <LayoutPanel graphic={graphic} editing={editing} themeOptions={themeOptions} onChangeGraphic={p => console.log('Graphic change', p)} onChangeLayoutDefinition={def => console.log('Layout definition change', def)} onClose={() => console.log('LayoutPanel closed')} />;
  }
```

<file path="design-system-charts-trendchart.txt">
# Design System/charts/TrendChart

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `initChart` | `(rootElement: string | HTMLDivElement, options: TrendChartInitOptions) => Promise<{ sciChartSurface: SciChartSurface; }>` | Yes |  |  |
| `showLegend` | `boolean` | No | true |  |
| `legendPlacement` | `"bottom" "left" "right" "top"` | No | bottom | Position of the legend relative to the chart. |
| `legendContainerId` | `string` | No |  |  |
| `sx` | `SxProps<Theme>` | No |  |  |
| `onError` | `((error: Error, info?: ErrorInfo) => void)` | No |  | Called on async init failure or render error. `info` is only set for render errors. |


## Examples


### Playground

**Playground** — configurable chart for testing multiple scenarios.

Use the preset buttons or the sliders to change the number of numeric /
string tags, toggle shared ranges, and exercise every combination of
Banded Axis and Merge Similar Axes.

```tsx
{
  decorators: [Story => <Box sx={{
    p: 2,
    height: 'calc(100vh - 32px)',
    bgcolor: 'grey.50',
    overflow: 'hidden',
    display: 'flex',
    flexDirection: 'column'
  }}>
        <Story />
      </Box>],
  render: () => {
    const PlaygroundWrapper = () => {
      const {
        surfaceRef,
        themeRef
      } = useChartTheme();
      const [config, setConfig] = useState<PlaygroundConfig>(PRESETS[0].config);
      const [chartKey, setChartKey] = useState(0);
      const [fps, setFps] = useState(0);
      const fpsMeterRef = useRef<{
        stop: () => void;
      } | null>(null);
      const [statsRange, setStatsRange] = useState<SelectedRange | null>(null);
      const [optionsValues, setOptionsValues] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
        BandedAxis: false,
        MergeSimilarAxes: false,
        UpdateOnNavigate: false,
        Statistics: false
      });

      // Detach the FPS meter when the playground unmounts.
      useEffect(() => () => fpsMeterRef.current?.stop(), []);
      const [graphicState, setGraphicState] = useState({
        showAllYAxis: false,
        showAllLines: true,
        showPointMarkers: false,
        showTodayLine: false,
        showGaps: false,
        showLegend: true,
        showLegendCheckboxes: false,
        showSeriesMarkers: true,
        legendPlacement: 3,
        legendSourceMode: sourceModeOptions[0].value,
        trendTheme: themeOptions[0].text
      });
      const numericTags = buildNumericTags(config.numericCount, config.sharedRange, config.numericRanges);
      const stringTags = buildStringTags(config.stringCount);
      const booleanTags = buildBooleanTags(config.booleanCount ?? 0);

      // Stash the current tags in refs so the SciChartReact remount triggered
      // by `chartKey` picks up the latest values without making `initChart`'s
      // identity flip on every render.
      const numericTagsRef = useRef(numericTags);
      const stringTagsRef = useRef(stringTags);
      const booleanTagsRef = useRef(booleanTags);
      const pointsPerTagRef = useRef(config.pointsPerTag ?? DEFAULT_POINTS_PER_TAG);
      numericTagsRef.current = numericTags;
      stringTagsRef.current = stringTags;
      booleanTagsRef.current = booleanTags;
      pointsPerTagRef.current = config.pointsPerTag ?? DEFAULT_POINTS_PER_TAG;
      const applyConfig = useCallback((next: PlaygroundConfig) => {
        setConfig(next);
        setOptionsValues({
          BandedAxis: false,
          MergeSimilarAxes: false,
          UpdateOnNavigate: false,
          Statistics: false
        });
        setStatsRange(null);
        setChartKey(k => k + 1);
      }, []);
      const handleChangeGraphic = useCallback((patch: Record<string, unknown>) => {
        setGraphicState(prev => {
          const next = {
            ...prev,
            ...patch
          };
          const surface = surfaceRef.current;
          if (!surface) return next;
          if ('showAllLines' in patch) setShowAllLines(surface, next.showAllLines);
          if ('showPointMarkers' in patch) setShowPointMarkers(surface, next.showPointMarkers);
          if ('showTodayLine' in patch) {
            setShowTodayLine(surface, next.showTodayLine, {
              todayXValue: getTodayMidnightMs()
            });
          }
          if ('trendTheme' in patch) {
            setTrendTheme(surface, next.trendTheme === 'Dark' ? DARK_THEME : LIGHT_THEME);
          }
          if ('showLegend' in patch) setShowLegend(surface, next.showLegend);
          if ('showLegendCheckboxes' in patch) setLegendCheckboxes(surface, next.showLegendCheckboxes);
          if ('showSeriesMarkers' in patch) setLegendSeriesMarkers(surface, next.showSeriesMarkers);
          return next;
        });
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const initChart = useCallback(async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
        legendContainerId
      }: TrendChartInitOptions) => {
        const {
          sciChartSurface,
          wasmContext
        } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
          theme: themeRef.current
        });
        surfaceRef.current = sciChartSurface;

        // Measure the interactive frame rate (pan/zoom) for perf comparisons.
        // Storybook-only helper: counts SciChart `rendered` events per second.
        fpsMeterRef.current?.stop();
        let frames = 0;
        const onRendered = (): void => {
          frames += 1;
        };
        sciChartSurface.rendered.subscribe(onRendered);
        const fpsTimer = window.setInterval(() => {
          setFps(frames);
          frames = 0;
        }, 1000);
        fpsMeterRef.current = {
          stop: () => {
            window.clearInterval(fpsTimer);
            sciChartSurface.rendered.unsubscribe(onRendered);
          }
        };
        sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildTimeXAxis(wasmContext, START, NOW));

        // Numeric axes & series
        numericTagsRef.current.forEach((tag, i) => {
          const yAxis = buildYAxis(wasmContext, tag.id, tag.name, tag.min, tag.max, tag.color);
          yAxis.drawMajorGridLines = i === 0;
          sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(yAxis);
          const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
          const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
          // Spread `pointsPerTag` points evenly across the visible window so a
          // higher count raises density (stress test) instead of the range.
          const pts = pointsPerTagRef.current;
          const step = Math.max(1, (NOW - START) / pts);
          const data = generateSineWave(START, pts, step, amplitude, offset, amplitude * 0.3 + i * 0.5);
          sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, `sn${i}`, tag.name, data, tag.color, tag.id, false, {
            engLow: tag.min,
            engHigh: tag.max
          }));
        });

        // Modifiers
        const stringAxisIds = new Set<string>();
        addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
          legendContainerId,
          stringAxisIds
        });

        // String axes & segments
        stringTagsRef.current.forEach(tag => {
          stringAxisIds.add(addStringAxis(sciChartSurface, wasmContext, tag));
          addStringSegments(sciChartSurface, tag.id, generateStringSegments(tag.name, START, NOW), tag.color);
        });

        // Boolean axes & segments — identical to string tags but unlabelled.
        booleanTagsRef.current.forEach(tag => {
          stringAxisIds.add(addStringAxis(sciChartSurface, wasmContext, tag));
          addBooleanSegments(sciChartSurface, tag.id, generateStringSegments(tag.name, START, NOW), tag.color);
        });

        // Update-on-Navigate (mocked fetch): rewrites every tag for the new
        // visible window. Toggled via the OptionsPanel; off by default.
        enableUpdateOnNavigate(sciChartSurface, {
          debounceMs: 200,
          onNavigate: ({
            min,
            max
          }) => {
            if (sciChartSurface.isDeleted) return;
            sciChartSurface.suspendUpdates();
            try {
              numericTagsRef.current.forEach((tag, i) => {
                const rs = sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.asArray().find(s => s.id === `sn${i}`);
                const ds = rs?.dataSeries;
                if (ds instanceof XyDataSeries) {
                  const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
                  const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
                  const data = mockTrendSeries(min, max, amplitude, offset, i * 0.8, pointsPerTagRef.current);
                  ds.clear();
                  ds.appendRange(data.x, data.y);
                }
              });
              const rebuild = (tag: {
                id: string;
                name: string;
                color: string;
              }, withLabels: boolean) => {
                sciChartSurface.annotations.asArray().filter(a => a.id.startsWith(`string-seg-${tag.id}`)).forEach(a => sciChartSurface.annotations.remove(a));
                const segments = generateStringSegments(tag.name, min, max);
                if (withLabels) addStringSegments(sciChartSurface, tag.id, segments, tag.color);else addBooleanSegments(sciChartSurface, tag.id, segments, tag.color);
              };
              stringTagsRef.current.forEach(tag => rebuild(tag, true));
              booleanTagsRef.current.forEach(tag => rebuild(tag, false));
            } finally {
              sciChartSurface.resumeUpdates();
            }
          }
        });

        // Statistics range-selection: drag to select an X window. The selected
        // range (start/end) is what a consumer would feed to a stats API call.
        enableRangeSelection(sciChartSurface, {
          onRangeChange: range => setStatsRange(range),
          onRangeSelected: range => setStatsRange(range)
        });
        return {
          sciChartSurface
        };
      }, [surfaceRef, themeRef]);
      const handleToggleOption = useCallback((key: string, next: boolean) => {
        const surface = surfaceRef.current;
        if (!surface || surface.isDeleted) return;
        setOptionsValues(prev => {
          const updated = {
            ...prev,
            [key]: next
          };
          if (key === 'MergeSimilarAxes') {
            setMergeSimilarAxes(surface, next);
            if (updated.BandedAxis) setBandedAxis(surface, true);
          }
          if (key === 'BandedAxis') {
            setBandedAxis(surface, next);
          }
          if (key === 'UpdateOnNavigate') {
            setUpdateOnNavigate(surface, next);
          }
          if (key === 'Statistics') {
            setRangeSelectionEnabled(surface, next);
            if (!next) setStatsRange(null);
          }
          return updated;
        });
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const handleDefaultZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) setDefaultZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const handleAutoZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) doAutoZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      return <Box sx={{
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        height: '100%',
        gap: 1
      }}>
          {/* Preset buttons */}
          <Box sx={{
          display: 'flex',
          gap: 1,
          flexWrap: 'wrap',
          alignItems: 'center'
        }}>
            {PRESETS.map(p => {
            const isActive = config.numericCount === p.config.numericCount && config.stringCount === p.config.stringCount && config.sharedRange === p.config.sharedRange && (config.booleanCount ?? 0) === (p.config.booleanCount ?? 0);
            return <Box key={p.label} component="button" onClick={() => applyConfig({
              ...p.config,
              pointsPerTag: config.pointsPerTag
            })} sx={{
              px: 1.5,
              py: 0.5,
              border: '1px solid',
              borderColor: isActive ? 'primary.main' : 'divider',
              borderRadius: 1,
              bgcolor: isActive ? 'primary.main' : 'background.paper',
              color: isActive ? 'primary.contrastText' : 'text.primary',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: 13,
              fontWeight: isActive ? 600 : 400,
              '&:hover': {
                bgcolor: isActive ? 'primary.dark' : 'action.hover'
              }
            }}>
                  {p.label}
                </Box>;
          })}

            <Box sx={{
            mx: 1,
            height: 24,
            borderLeft: '1px solid',
            borderColor: 'divider'
          }} />

            {/* Custom sliders */}
            <Box sx={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: 0.5,
            fontSize: 13
          }}>
              Numeric:
              <TextField type="number" size="small" value={config.numericCount} onChange={e => applyConfig({
              ...config,
              numericCount: Math.max(0, Math.min(10, Number(e.target.value)))
            })} slotProps={{
              htmlInput: {
                min: 0,
                max: 10,
                style: {
                  width: 48,
                  textAlign: 'center'
                }
              }
            }} />
            </Box>
            <Box sx={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: 0.5,
            fontSize: 13
          }}>
              String:
              <TextField type="number" size="small" value={config.stringCount} onChange={e => applyConfig({
              ...config,
              stringCount: Math.max(0, Math.min(5, Number(e.target.value)))
            })} slotProps={{
              htmlInput: {
                min: 0,
                max: 5,
                style: {
                  width: 48,
                  textAlign: 'center'
                }
              }
            }} />
            </Box>
            <Box sx={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: 0.5,
            fontSize: 13
          }}>
              Boolean:
              <TextField type="number" size="small" value={config.booleanCount ?? 0} onChange={e => applyConfig({
              ...config,
              booleanCount: Math.max(0, Math.min(5, Number(e.target.value)))
            })} slotProps={{
              htmlInput: {
                min: 0,
                max: 5,
                style: {
                  width: 48,
                  textAlign: 'center'
                }
              }
            }} />
            </Box>
            <Box component="label" sx={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: 0.5,
            fontSize: 13,
            cursor: 'pointer'
          }}>
              <input type="checkbox" checked={config.sharedRange} onChange={e => applyConfig({
              ...config,
              sharedRange: e.target.checked
            })} />
              Shared ranges
            </Box>

            <Box sx={{
            mx: 1,
            height: 24,
            borderLeft: '1px solid',
            borderColor: 'divider'
          }} />

            {/* Stress test: points per numeric tag */}
            <Box sx={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: 0.5,
            fontSize: 13
          }}>
              Points/tag:
              <TextField select size="small" value={config.pointsPerTag ?? DEFAULT_POINTS_PER_TAG} onChange={e => applyConfig({
              ...config,
              pointsPerTag: Number(e.target.value)
            })} slotProps={{
              select: {
                native: true
              }
            }} sx={{
              '& select': {
                py: 0.5,
                fontSize: 13
              }
            }}>
                {(POINTS_PER_TAG_OPTIONS.includes(DEFAULT_POINTS_PER_TAG) ? POINTS_PER_TAG_OPTIONS : [DEFAULT_POINTS_PER_TAG, ...POINTS_PER_TAG_OPTIONS]).map(n => <option key={n} value={n}>
                    {n.toLocaleString()}
                  </option>)}
              </TextField>
            </Box>
          </Box>

          {/* Chart */}
          <Box sx={{
          flex: 1,
          minHeight: 0
        }}>
            <ChartCard>
              <ChartCard.Header>
                <ChartCard.Header.LayoutPanel graphic={graphicState} editing={{
                layoutDefinition: layoutDefinitions[0].value,
                layoutDefinitions,
                isBandedAxis: optionsValues.BandedAxis ?? false,
                loadingPreferences: false,
                preferencesError: null,
                isSaving: false,
                saveError: null,
                saveSuccess: false,
                hasUnsavedChanges: false
              }} placementOptions={placementOptions} sourceModeOptions={sourceModeOptions} themeOptions={themeOptions} onChangeGraphic={handleChangeGraphic} onChangeLayoutDefinition={(def: string) => console.log('Layout definition change', def)} onSave={() => console.log('Save clicked')} onCancel={() => console.log('Cancel clicked')} onClose={() => console.log('LayoutPanel closed')} togglesConfig={[{
                key: 'showAllLines'
              }, {
                key: 'showPointMarkers'
              }, {
                key: 'showTodayLine'
              }, {
                key: 'showLegend'
              }, {
                key: 'showLegendCheckboxes',
                dependsOn: 'showLegend'
              }, {
                key: 'showSeriesMarkers',
                dependsOn: 'showLegend'
              }]} />
                <ChartCard.Header.OptionsPanel items={[{
                key: 'BandedAxis',
                label: 'Banded Axis'
              }, {
                key: 'MergeSimilarAxes',
                label: 'Merge Similar Axes'
              }, {
                key: 'UpdateOnNavigate',
                label: 'Update on Navigate'
              }, {
                key: 'Statistics',
                label: 'Statistics'
              }]} values={optionsValues} onToggle={handleToggleOption} />
                <ChartCard.Header.ChartControllers actionProps={{
                autoZoom: {
                  onClick: handleAutoZoom
                },
                defaultZoom: {
                  onClick: handleDefaultZoom
                }
              }} />
              </ChartCard.Header>
              <ChartCard.Body sx={{
              bgcolor: 'var(--background-paper-0, #f8f9fa)',
              flexDirection: 'column'
            }}>
                <TrendChart key={chartKey} initChart={initChart} showLegend={graphicState.showLegend} legendPlacement={toLegendPlacement(graphicState.legendPlacement)} />
              </ChartCard.Body>
            </ChartCard>
          </Box>

          {/* Info bar */}
          <Box sx={{
          fontSize: 12,
          color: 'text.secondary',
          px: 1
        }}>
            {numericTags.length} numeric tag{numericTags.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
            {config.sharedRange ? ' (shared 0–100)' : ' (unique ranges)'}
            {' · '}
            {stringTags.length} string tag{stringTags.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
            {' · '}
            {booleanTags.length} boolean tag{booleanTags.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
            {' · '}
            <Box component="span" sx={{
            fontWeight: 600,
            color: fps > 0 && fps < 30 ? 'warning.main' : 'success.main'
          }}>
              {fps} FPS
            </Box>{' '}
            (pan/zoom to measure)
            {optionsValues.Statistics ? <>
                {' · '}
                <Box component="span" sx={{
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: 'primary.main'
            }}>
                  {statsRange ? `Selected: ${new Date(statsRange.min).toLocaleString()} → ${new Date(statsRange.max).toLocaleString()}` : 'Statistics on — drag on the chart to select a range'}
                </Box>
              </> : null}
          </Box>
        </Box>;
    };
    return <PlaygroundWrapper />;
  }
}
```

### Roll Length

Numeric X-axis with roll length in meters (0m – 500m).

```tsx
{
  decorators: boundedStoryDecorator,
  render: () => {
    const thickness = generateNumericSeries(0, 500, 250, 0.08, 2.5, 0.03);
    const moisture = generateNumericSeries(0, 500, 250, 1.5, 6, 0.5);
    const buildChart = async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
      legendContainerId,
      theme,
      registerSurface
    }: StoryChartContext) => {
      const {
        sciChartSurface,
        wasmContext
      } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
        theme
      });
      registerSurface(sciChartSurface);
      sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildNumericXAxis(wasmContext, 0, 500, 'Roll Length', 'm'));
      sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(buildYAxis(wasmContext, 'y1', 'Thickness (mm)', 2, 3, '#1976d2'));
      sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(buildYAxis(wasmContext, 'y2', 'Moisture (%)', 0, 12, '#ed6c02'));
      const rollFmt = (v: number) => `${v.toFixed(1)} m`;

        engUnit: 'mm',
        displayOptions: {

        }

        displayOptions: {
          formatXValue: rollFmt
        }
      }));
      addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
        legendContainerId
      });
      return {
        sciChartSurface
      };
    };
    return <SimpleStoryChart buildChart={buildChart} />;
  }
}
```

### Update On Navigate

**UpdateOnNavigate** — the toggle sits next to Merge / Banded. When **on**,
navigating (zoom, pan, or the step back/forward buttons) refetches data for
the new visible window and rewrites every tag — numeric, string and boolean —
so only the visible range holds data (older out-of-range data drops out).
When **off**, navigation just moves the window and areas with no loaded data
show blank.

The fetch is mocked: synthetic data is regenerated for the requested range.

### Consuming it (with a real API)

Call `enableUpdateOnNavigate` once after `addDefaultModifiers`, then flip it
with `setUpdateOnNavigate` (or `applyTrendInitialState({ updateOnNavigate })`):

```tsx
enableUpdateOnNavigate(surface, {
  enabled: true,
  debounceMs: 300,
  // min/max are in the X-axis unit (ms for datetime). Fired debounced on
  // every zoom / pan / step.
  onNavigate: async ({ min, max }) => {
    const data = await api.fetchTrend({ from: min, to: max });

    surface.suspendUpdates();
    try {
      // Numeric: clear + repopulate only the visible window.
      data.numeric.forEach(({ seriesId, x, y }) => {
        const ds = surface.renderableSeries.getById(seriesId)?.dataSeries as XyDataSeries;
        ds?.clear();
        ds?.appendRange(x, y);
      });

      // String / boolean: drop the axis bands and re-add them for the range.
      data.bands.forEach(({ axisId, color, segments, isBoolean }) => {
        surface.annotations
          .asArray()
          .filter((a) => a.id.startsWith(`string-seg-${axisId}`))
          .forEach((a) => surface.annotations.remove(a));
        (isBoolean ? addBooleanSegments : addStringSegments)(surface, axisId, segments, color);
      });
    } finally {
      surface.resumeUpdates();
    }
  },
});

// Toggle at runtime (e.g. from the OptionsPanel):
setUpdateOnNavigate(surface, enabled);
```

```tsx
{
  decorators: boundedStoryDecorator,
  render: () => {
    const Wrapper = () => {
      const {
        surfaceRef,
        themeRef
      } = useChartTheme();
      const [optionsValues, setOptionsValues] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
        BandedAxis: false,
        MergeSimilarAxes: false,
        UpdateOnNavigate: true
      });

      // Mocked "fetch": regenerate data spanning only [min, max] and rewrite
      // each series / segment so the visible window is the only populated range.
      const refetchForRange = useCallback((min: number, max: number) => {
        const surface = surfaceRef.current;
        if (!surface || surface.isDeleted) return;

        // Batch the whole rewrite into a single repaint.
        surface.suspendUpdates();
        try {
          UON_NUMERIC_TAGS.forEach((tag, i) => {
            const rs = surface.renderableSeries.asArray().find(s => s.id === `sn${i}`);
            const ds = rs?.dataSeries;
            if (ds instanceof XyDataSeries) {
              const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
              const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
              const data = mockTrendSeries(min, max, amplitude, offset, i * 0.8);
              ds.clear();
              ds.appendRange(data.x, data.y);
            }
          });
          const rebuildSegments = (axisId: string, name: string, color: string, withLabels: boolean) => {
            surface.annotations.asArray().filter(a => a.id.startsWith(`string-seg-${axisId}`)).forEach(a => surface.annotations.remove(a));
            const segments = generateStringSegments(name, min, max);
            if (withLabels) addStringSegments(surface, axisId, segments, color);else addBooleanSegments(surface, axisId, segments, color);
          };
          rebuildSegments(UON_STRING_TAG.id, UON_STRING_TAG.name, UON_STRING_TAG.color, true);
          rebuildSegments(UON_BOOLEAN_TAG.id, UON_BOOLEAN_TAG.name, UON_BOOLEAN_TAG.color, false);
        } finally {
          surface.resumeUpdates();
        }
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const initChart = useCallback(async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
        legendContainerId
      }: TrendChartInitOptions) => {
        const {
          sciChartSurface,
          wasmContext
        } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
          theme: themeRef.current
        });
        surfaceRef.current = sciChartSurface;
        sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildTimeXAxis(wasmContext, START, NOW));
        UON_NUMERIC_TAGS.forEach((tag, i) => {
          const yAxis = buildYAxis(wasmContext, tag.id, tag.name, tag.min, tag.max, tag.color);
          yAxis.drawMajorGridLines = i === 0;
          sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(yAxis);
          const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
          const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
          const data = mockTrendSeries(START, NOW, amplitude, offset, i * 0.8);
          sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, `sn${i}`, tag.name, data, tag.color, tag.id, false, {
            engLow: tag.min,
            engHigh: tag.max
          }));
        });
        const stringAxisIds = new Set<string>();
        addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
          legendContainerId,
          stringAxisIds
        });
        stringAxisIds.add(addStringAxis(sciChartSurface, wasmContext, UON_STRING_TAG));
        addStringSegments(sciChartSurface, UON_STRING_TAG.id, generateStringSegments(UON_STRING_TAG.name, START, NOW), UON_STRING_TAG.color);
        stringAxisIds.add(addStringAxis(sciChartSurface, wasmContext, UON_BOOLEAN_TAG));
        addBooleanSegments(sciChartSurface, UON_BOOLEAN_TAG.id, generateStringSegments(UON_BOOLEAN_TAG.name, START, NOW), UON_BOOLEAN_TAG.color);

        // Register the (mocked) fetch hook; the OptionsPanel toggle flips it.
        enableUpdateOnNavigate(sciChartSurface, {
          enabled: true,
          debounceMs: 200,
          onNavigate: ({
            min,
            max
          }) => refetchForRange(min, max)
        });
        return {
          sciChartSurface
        };
      }, [surfaceRef, themeRef, refetchForRange]);
      const handleToggleOption = useCallback((key: string, next: boolean) => {
        const surface = surfaceRef.current;
        if (!surface || surface.isDeleted) return;
        setOptionsValues(prev => {
          const updated = {
            ...prev,
            [key]: next
          };
          if (key === 'MergeSimilarAxes') {
            setMergeSimilarAxes(surface, next);
            if (updated.BandedAxis) setBandedAxis(surface, true);
          }
          if (key === 'BandedAxis') setBandedAxis(surface, next);
          if (key === 'UpdateOnNavigate') setUpdateOnNavigate(surface, next);
          return updated;
        });
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      return <ChartCard>
          <ChartCard.Header>
            <ChartCard.Header.OptionsPanel items={[{
            key: 'BandedAxis',
            label: 'Banded Axis'
          }, {
            key: 'MergeSimilarAxes',
            label: 'Merge Similar Axes'
          }, {
            key: 'UpdateOnNavigate',
            label: 'Update on Navigate'
          }]} values={optionsValues} onToggle={handleToggleOption} />
            <ChartCard.Header.ChartControllers />
          </ChartCard.Header>
          <ChartCard.Body sx={{
          bgcolor: 'var(--background-paper-0, #f8f9fa)',
          flexDirection: 'column'
        }}>
            <TrendChart initChart={initChart} showLegend />
          </ChartCard.Body>
        </ChartCard>;
    };
    return <Wrapper />;
  }
}
```
