### Full Chart Card With Panels

Full ChartCard with LayoutPanel, InteractionsPanel, OptionsPanel,
UndoRedo and ChartControllers — the complete header configuration
as used in Product Tracker / Data Analytics.

The Graphic tab toggles are **functional**: Show all lines, Show point
markers (7×7 circles), Show today line (dashed vertical annotation),
and Theme (light / dark) all modify the SciChart surface in real time.

```tsx
{
  decorators: [Story => <Box sx={{
    p: 3,
    height: 'calc(100vh - 32px)',
    bgcolor: 'grey.50',
    overflow: 'hidden'
  }}>
        <Story />
      </Box>],
  render: () => {
    const PRESETS: {
      label: string;
      ms: number;
    }[] = [{
      label: 'Last 30 minutes',
      ms: 30 * 60_000
    }, {
      label: 'Last 1 hour',
      ms: HOUR
    }, {
      label: 'Last 4 hours',
      ms: 4 * HOUR
    }, {
      label: 'Last 8 hours',
      ms: 8 * HOUR
    }, {
      label: 'Last 24 hours',
      ms: DAY
    }, {
      label: 'Last 3 days',
      ms: 3 * DAY
    }, {
      label: 'Last 7 days',
      ms: 7 * DAY
    }, {
      label: 'Last 30 days',
      ms: 30 * DAY
    }, {
      label: 'Custom',
      ms: 0
    }];
    const Wrapper = () => {
      const {
        surfaceRef,
        themeRef
      } = useChartTheme();
      const [optionsValues, setOptionsValues] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
      const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState(START);
      const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState(NOW);
      const [preset, setPreset] = useState('Last 7 days');

      // --- Graphic state (toggles + theme) -----------------------------------
      const [graphicState, setGraphicState] = useState({
        showAllYAxis: false,
        showAllLines: true,
        showPointMarkers: false,
        showTodayLine: false,
        showGaps: false,
        showLegend: true,
        showLegendCheckboxes: false,
        showSeriesMarkers: true,
        legendPlacement: 3,
        legendSourceMode: sourceModeOptions[0].value,
        trendTheme: themeOptions[0].text
      });

      // --- Interaction state (zoom/pan, rollover, mouse wheel) ----------------
      const [interactionValues, setInteractionValues] = useState<InteractionValues>({
        zoomXAxisOnly: true,
        executeZoomOn: 2,
        // Right button (matches addDefaultModifiers)
        executePanOn: 0,
        // Left button (matches addDefaultModifiers)
        panningDirection: 0,
        // X (matches addDefaultModifiers)
        rolloverEnabled: true,
        rolloverShowDescription: true,
        rolloverShowName: true,
        rolloverTooltipOn: 1,
        // Always
        showTooltipOn: 1,
        // Mouse Over
        mouseWheelEnabled: true,
        mouseWheelAction: 0,
        // Zoom
        mouseWheelDirection: 0 // XDirection (matches addDefaultModifiers)
      });
      const kpiData = generateSineWave(START, 200, HOUR, 30, 55, 5);
      const varData = generateRandomWalk(START, 200, HOUR, 15, 1.2);
      const initChart = useCallback(async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
        legendContainerId
      }: TrendChartInitOptions) => {
        const {
          sciChartSurface,
          wasmContext
        } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
          theme: themeRef.current
        });
        surfaceRef.current = sciChartSurface;
        sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildTimeXAxis(wasmContext, START, NOW));
        sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(buildYAxis(wasmContext, 'y1', 'Production KPI', 0, 100, '#1976d2'));
        sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(buildYAxis(wasmContext, 'y2', 'Variable', 0, 30, '#ed6c02'));
        sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, 's1', 'Production KPI', kpiData, '#1976d2', 'y1'));
        sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, 's2', 'Variable', varData, '#ed6c02', 'y2'));
        addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
          legendContainerId
        });
        return {
          sciChartSurface
        };
      }, [kpiData, varData, surfaceRef, themeRef]);

      // --- Sync date pickers after a range shift ---
      const syncDates = useCallback((result: {
        start: number;
        end: number;
      } | undefined) => {
        if (result) {
          setStartDate(result.start);
          setEndDate(result.end);
          setPreset('Custom');
        }
      }, []);

      // --- Apply graphic changes to the live SciChart surface -----------------
      const handleChangeGraphic = useCallback((patch: Record<string, unknown>) => {
        setGraphicState(prev => {
          const next = {
            ...prev,
            ...patch
          };
          const surface = surfaceRef.current;
          if (!surface) return next;
          if ('showAllLines' in patch) {
            setShowAllLines(surface, next.showAllLines);
          }
          if ('showPointMarkers' in patch) {
            setShowPointMarkers(surface, next.showPointMarkers);
          }
          if ('showTodayLine' in patch) {
            setShowTodayLine(surface, next.showTodayLine, {
              todayXValue: getTodayMidnightMs()
            });
          }
          if ('trendTheme' in patch) {
            setTrendTheme(surface, next.trendTheme === 'Dark' ? DARK_THEME : LIGHT_THEME);
          }
          if ('showLegend' in patch) {
            setShowLegend(surface, next.showLegend);
          }
          if ('showLegendCheckboxes' in patch) {
            setLegendCheckboxes(surface, next.showLegendCheckboxes);
          }
          if ('showSeriesMarkers' in patch) {
            setLegendSeriesMarkers(surface, next.showSeriesMarkers);
          }
          return next;
        });
      }, [surfaceRef]);

      // --- Apply interaction changes to the live SciChart surface ---------------
      const handleChangeInteractions = useCallback((patch: Partial<InteractionValues>) => {
        setInteractionValues(prev => {
          let next = {
            ...prev,
            ...patch
          };

          // Resolve zoom/pan button conflicts
          if ('executeZoomOn' in patch || 'executePanOn' in patch) {
            const changed = 'executeZoomOn' in patch ? 'executeZoomOn' : 'executePanOn';
            const resolved = resolveZoomPanConflict(next, changed);
            next = {
              ...next,
              ...resolved
            };
          }
          const surface = surfaceRef.current;
          if (surface) applyInteractionValues(surface, next);
          return next;
        });
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const handleDefaultZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (!surfaceRef.current) return;
        setDefaultZoom(surfaceRef.current);
        setStartDate(START);
        setEndDate(NOW);
        setPreset('Last 7 days');
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const handleAutoZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) doAutoZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      return <ChartCard>
          <ChartCard.HeaderArea sx={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: 2,
          px: 3,
          py: 1.5,
          borderBottom: '1px solid',
          borderColor: 'divider'
        }}>
            <DateTimePicker label="Start" value={dayjs(startDate)} onChange={(value: Dayjs | null) => {
            if (!value || !surfaceRef.current) return;
            const ms = value.valueOf();
            setStartDate(ms);
            setPreset('Custom');
            setXRange(surfaceRef.current, ms, endDate);
          }} slotProps={{
            textField: {
              size: 'small',
              sx: {
                width: 200
              }
            }
          }} />
            <Box sx={{
            color: 'text.secondary',
            fontSize: 14,
            userSelect: 'none'
          }}>—</Box>
            <DateTimePicker label="End" value={dayjs(endDate)} minDateTime={dayjs(startDate)} onChange={(value: Dayjs | null) => {
            if (!value || !surfaceRef.current) return;
            const ms = value.valueOf();
            setEndDate(ms);
            setPreset('Custom');
            setXRange(surfaceRef.current, startDate, ms);
          }} slotProps={{
            textField: {
              size: 'small',
              sx: {
                width: 200
              }
            }
          }} />
            <TextField select label="Preset" size="small" value={preset} onChange={e => {
            const selected = PRESETS.find(p => p.label === e.target.value);
            if (!selected || selected.ms === 0) {
              setPreset('Custom');
              return;
            }
            setPreset(selected.label);
            const newEnd = Date.now();
            const newStart = newEnd - selected.ms;
            setStartDate(newStart);
            setEndDate(newEnd);
            if (surfaceRef.current) setXRange(surfaceRef.current, newStart, newEnd);
          }} sx={{
            width: 180
          }}>
              {PRESETS.map(p => <MenuItem key={p.label} value={p.label}>
                  {p.label}
                </MenuItem>)}
            </TextField>
          </ChartCard.HeaderArea>
          <ChartCard.Header>
            <ChartCard.Header.LayoutPanel graphic={graphicState} editing={{
            layoutDefinition: layoutDefinitions[0].value,
            layoutDefinitions,
            isBandedAxis: false,
            loadingPreferences: false,
            preferencesError: null,
            isSaving: false,
            saveError: null,
            saveSuccess: false,
            hasUnsavedChanges: false
          }} placementOptions={placementOptions} sourceModeOptions={sourceModeOptions} themeOptions={themeOptions} onChangeGraphic={handleChangeGraphic} onChangeLayoutDefinition={(def: string) => console.log('Layout definition change', def)} onSave={() => console.log('Save clicked')} onCancel={() => console.log('Cancel clicked')} onClose={() => console.log('LayoutPanel closed')} labels={{
            layoutSource: 'Layout Source',
            tabs: {
              graphic: 'Graphic',
              legend: 'Legend'
            },
            save: 'Save'
          }} togglesConfig={[{
            key: 'showAllLines'
          }, {
            key: 'showPointMarkers'
          }, {
            key: 'showTodayLine'
          }, {
            key: 'showLegend'
          }, {
            key: 'showLegendCheckboxes',
            dependsOn: 'showLegend'
          }, {
            key: 'showSeriesMarkers',
            dependsOn: 'showLegend'
          }]} />
            <ChartCard.Header.InteractionsPanel values={interactionValues} editing={{
            layoutDefinition: layoutDefinitions[0].value,
            layoutDefinitions,
            isSaving: false,
            saveError: null,
            saveSuccess: false,
            hasUnsavedChanges: false
          }} zoomOnOptions={zoomOnOptions} panOnOptions={panOnOptions} panningDirectionOptions={panningDirectionOptions} showTooltipOnOptions={showTooltipOnOptions} rolloverTooltipOnOptions={rolloverTooltipOnOptions} mouseWheelActionOptions={mouseWheelActionOptions} mouseWheelDirectionOptions={mouseWheelDirectionOptions} onChangeValues={handleChangeInteractions} onChangeLayoutDefinition={(def: string) => console.log('Layout def change', def)} onSave={() => console.log('Save clicked')} onCancel={() => console.log('Cancel clicked')} onClose={() => console.log('Close panel')} />
            <ChartCard.Header.OptionsPanel items={[{
            key: 'BandedAxis',
            label: 'Banded Axis'
          }, {
            key: 'MergeSimilarAxes',
            label: 'Merge Similar Axes'
          }]} values={optionsValues} onToggle={(key: string, next: boolean) => {
            setOptionsValues((prev: Record<string, boolean>) => {
              const updated = {
                ...prev,
                [key]: next
              };
              const surface = surfaceRef.current;
              if (surface) {
                setMergeSimilarAxes(surface, updated.MergeSimilarAxes ?? false);
                setBandedAxis(surface, updated.BandedAxis ?? false);
              }
              return updated;
            });
          }} />
            <ChartCard.Header.UndoRedo actionProps={{
            undo: {
              onClick: () => console.log('Undo')
            },
            redo: {
              onClick: () => console.log('Redo')
            }
          }} />
            <ChartCard.Header.ChartControllers actionProps={{
            fullRangeBack: {
              onClick: () => {
                if (surfaceRef.current) syncDates(shiftXRange(surfaceRef.current, -1));
              }
            },
            halfRangeBack: {
              onClick: () => {
                if (surfaceRef.current) syncDates(shiftXRange(surfaceRef.current, -0.5));
              }
            },
            halfRangeForward: {
              onClick: () => {
                if (surfaceRef.current) syncDates(shiftXRange(surfaceRef.current, 0.5));
              }
            },
            fullRangeForward: {
              onClick: () => {
                if (surfaceRef.current) syncDates(shiftXRange(surfaceRef.current, 1));
              }
            },
            autoZoom: {
              onClick: handleAutoZoom
            },
            defaultZoom: {
              onClick: handleDefaultZoom
            }
          }} />
          </ChartCard.Header>
          <ChartCard.Body sx={{
          bgcolor: 'var(--background-paper-0, #f8f9fa)',
          flexDirection: 'column'
        }}>
            <TrendChart initChart={initChart} showLegend={graphicState.showLegend} legendPlacement={toLegendPlacement(graphicState.legendPlacement)} />
          </ChartCard.Body>
        </ChartCard>;
    };
    return <Wrapper />;
  }
}
```

### Dynamic Tag Addition

**DynamicTagAddition** — demonstrates adding tags to an existing chart at
runtime without recreating the surface.

A collapsible sidebar drawer lists available numeric and string tags, each
with a "+" button and drag-and-drop support. Dropping a tag onto the chart
(or clicking "+") calls `addNumericSeries` / `addStringSeries` to inject
the new axis and data into the live surface.

Starts with 2 numeric + 1 string tag already on the chart.

```tsx
{
  decorators: [Story => <Box sx={{
    p: 2,
    height: 'calc(100vh - 32px)',
    bgcolor: 'grey.50',
    overflow: 'hidden',
    display: 'flex',
    flexDirection: 'column'
  }}>
        <Story />
      </Box>],
  render: () => {
    const initialNumericTags = [{
      id: 'yn0',
      name: 'Temperature (°C)',
      color: '#1976d2',
      min: 0,
      max: 100
    }, {
      id: 'yn1',
      name: 'Pressure (bar)',
      color: '#ed6c02',
      min: 0,
      max: 200
    }];
    const initialStringTags = [{
      id: 'ys0',
      name: 'Machine Status',
      color: '#0288d1'
    }];
    const DynamicWrapper = () => {
      const {
        surfaceRef,
        themeRef
      } = useChartTheme();
      const wasmRef = useRef<TSciChart | null>(null);
      const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(true);
      const [addedIds, setAddedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
      const [dropHighlight, setDropHighlight] = useState(false);
      const [optionsValues, setOptionsValues] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
        BandedAxis: false,
        MergeSimilarAxes: false
      });
      const handleAddNumericTag = useCallback((tag: (typeof AVAILABLE_NUMERIC_TAGS)[number]) => {
        const surface = surfaceRef.current;
        const wasm = wasmRef.current;
        if (!surface || surface.isDeleted || !wasm) return;
        if (addedIds.has(tag.id)) return;
        const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
        const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
        const data = generateSineWave(START, 200, HOUR, amplitude, offset, amplitude * 0.3);
        addNumericSeries(surface, wasm, {
          id: tag.id,
          name: tag.name,
          color: tag.color,
          xValues: data.x,
          yValues: data.y,
          visibleRange: {
            min: tag.min,
            max: tag.max
          },
          engLow: tag.min,
          engHigh: tag.max
        });
        setAddedIds(prev => new Set(prev).add(tag.id));
      }, [surfaceRef, addedIds]);
      const handleAddStringTag = useCallback((tag: (typeof AVAILABLE_STRING_TAGS)[number]) => {
        const surface = surfaceRef.current;
        const wasm = wasmRef.current;
        if (!surface || surface.isDeleted || !wasm) return;
        if (addedIds.has(tag.id)) return;
        addStringSeries(surface, wasm, {
          axis: tag,
          segments: generateStringSegments(tag.name, START, NOW)
        });
        setAddedIds(prev => new Set(prev).add(tag.id));
      }, [surfaceRef, addedIds]);
      const handleDrop = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setDropHighlight(false);
        const raw = e.dataTransfer.getData(DRAG_TYPE);
        if (!raw) return;
        const {
          id,
          kind
        } = JSON.parse(raw) as {
          id: string;
          kind: 'numeric' | 'string';
        };
        if (kind === 'numeric') {
          const tag = AVAILABLE_NUMERIC_TAGS.find(t => t.id === id);
          if (tag) handleAddNumericTag(tag);
        } else {
          const tag = AVAILABLE_STRING_TAGS.find(t => t.id === id);
          if (tag) handleAddStringTag(tag);
        }
      }, [handleAddNumericTag, handleAddStringTag]);
      const handleDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {
        if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes(DRAG_TYPE)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
          setDropHighlight(true);
        }
      }, []);
      const handleDragLeave = useCallback(() => setDropHighlight(false), []);
      const initChart = useCallback(async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
        legendContainerId
      }: TrendChartInitOptions) => {
        const {
          sciChartSurface,
          wasmContext
        } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
          theme: themeRef.current
        });
        surfaceRef.current = sciChartSurface;
        wasmRef.current = wasmContext;
        sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildTimeXAxis(wasmContext, START, NOW));
        initialNumericTags.forEach((tag, i) => {
          const yAxis = buildYAxis(wasmContext, tag.id, tag.name, tag.min, tag.max, tag.color);
          yAxis.drawMajorGridLines = i === 0;
          sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(yAxis);
          const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
          const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
          const data = generateSineWave(START, 200, HOUR, amplitude, offset, amplitude * 0.3);
          sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, `sn${i}`, tag.name, data, tag.color, tag.id, false, {
            engLow: tag.min,
            engHigh: tag.max
          }));
        });
        const stringAxisIds = new Set<string>();
        addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
          legendContainerId,
          stringAxisIds
        });
        initialStringTags.forEach(tag => {
          stringAxisIds.add(addStringAxis(sciChartSurface, wasmContext, tag));
          addStringSegments(sciChartSurface, tag.id, generateStringSegments(tag.name, START, NOW), tag.color);
        });
        return {
          sciChartSurface
        };
      }, [surfaceRef, themeRef]);
      const handleToggleOption = useCallback((key: string, next: boolean) => {
        const surface = surfaceRef.current;
        if (!surface || surface.isDeleted) return;
        setOptionsValues(prev => {
          const updated = {
            ...prev,
            [key]: next
          };
          if (key === 'MergeSimilarAxes') {
            setMergeSimilarAxes(surface, next);
            if (updated.BandedAxis) setBandedAxis(surface, true);
          }
          if (key === 'BandedAxis') {
            setBandedAxis(surface, next);
          }
          if (key === 'UpdateOnNavigate') {
            setUpdateOnNavigate(surface, next);
          }
          return updated;
        });
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const handleDefaultZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) setDefaultZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const handleAutoZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) doAutoZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      return <Box sx={{
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100%',
        gap: 1
      }}>
          <Box sx={{
          width: drawerOpen ? 260 : 40,
          flexShrink: 0,
          transition: 'width 0.25s ease',
          overflow: 'hidden',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          bgcolor: 'background.paper',
          borderRadius: 1,
          border: '1px solid',
          borderColor: 'divider'
        }}>
            <Box sx={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: drawerOpen ? 'space-between' : 'center',
            px: drawerOpen ? 1.5 : 0,
            py: 1,
            borderBottom: '1px solid',
            borderColor: 'divider',
            minHeight: 40
          }}>
              {drawerOpen && <Box sx={{
              fontSize: 13,
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: 'text.primary'
            }}>Available Tags</Box>}
              <Box component="button" onClick={() => setDrawerOpen(o => !o)} sx={{
              width: 28,
              height: 28,
              border: 'none',
              bgcolor: 'transparent',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              fontSize: 18,
              color: 'text.secondary',
              borderRadius: 1,
              p: 0,
              '&:hover': {
                bgcolor: 'action.hover'
              }
            }}>
                {drawerOpen ? '◀' : '▶'}
              </Box>
            </Box>

            {drawerOpen && <Box sx={{
            flex: 1,
            overflowY: 'auto',
            py: 1
          }}>
                <Box sx={{
              px: 1.5,
              py: 0.5,
              fontSize: 11,
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: 'text.secondary',
              textTransform: 'uppercase',
              letterSpacing: 0.5
            }}>
                  Numeric Tags
                </Box>
                {AVAILABLE_NUMERIC_TAGS.map(tag => <TagRow key={tag.id} tag={tag} kind="numeric" disabled={addedIds.has(tag.id)} onAdd={() => handleAddNumericTag(tag)} />)}

                <Box sx={{
              my: 1,
              borderBottom: '1px solid',
              borderColor: 'divider',
              mx: 1.5
            }} />

                <Box sx={{
              px: 1.5,
              py: 0.5,
              fontSize: 11,
              fontWeight: 600,
              color: 'text.secondary',
              textTransform: 'uppercase',
              letterSpacing: 0.5
            }}>
                  String Tags
                </Box>
                {AVAILABLE_STRING_TAGS.map(tag => <TagRow key={tag.id} tag={tag} kind="string" disabled={addedIds.has(tag.id)} onAdd={() => handleAddStringTag(tag)} />)}
              </Box>}
          </Box>

          <Box sx={{
          flex: 1,
          minWidth: 0,
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column'
        }} onDrop={handleDrop} onDragOver={handleDragOver} onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}>
            <ChartCard>
              <ChartCard.Header>
                <ChartCard.Header.OptionsPanel items={[{
                key: 'BandedAxis',
                label: 'Banded Axis'
              }, {
                key: 'MergeSimilarAxes',
                label: 'Merge Similar Axes'
              }]} values={optionsValues} onToggle={handleToggleOption} />
                <ChartCard.Header.ChartControllers actionProps={{
                autoZoom: {
                  onClick: handleAutoZoom
                },
                defaultZoom: {
                  onClick: handleDefaultZoom
                }
              }} />
              </ChartCard.Header>
              <ChartCard.Body sx={{
              bgcolor: 'var(--background-paper-0, #f8f9fa)',
              flexDirection: 'column',
              position: 'relative',
              outline: dropHighlight ? '2px dashed' : 'none',
              outlineColor: 'primary.main',
              outlineOffset: -2,
              transition: 'outline 0.15s'
            }}>
                <TrendChart initChart={initChart} showLegend />
                {dropHighlight && <Box sx={{
                position: 'absolute',
                inset: 0,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                bgcolor: 'rgba(25, 118, 210, 0.06)',
                pointerEvents: 'none',
                zIndex: 10,
                borderRadius: 1
              }}>
                    <Box sx={{
                  px: 3,
                  py: 1.5,
                  bgcolor: 'primary.main',
                  color: 'primary.contrastText',
                  borderRadius: 2,
                  fontSize: 14,
                  fontWeight: 600
                }}>
                      Drop tag to add to chart
                    </Box>
                  </Box>}
              </ChartCard.Body>
            </ChartCard>
          </Box>
        </Box>;
    };
    return <DynamicWrapper />;
  }
}
```

### Tag Strings

Tag Strings — hybrid layout with string axes stacked at the top
and numeric axes sharing the bottom area.

- 3 numeric tags: Temperature, Pressure, Flow Rate
- 3 string tags: Machine Status, Quality Grade, Operator Shift

Uses HybridLeftAxisLayoutStrategy to position string axes in
individual rows at the top (~12 % each, capped at 40 % total)
while numeric axes share the remaining space with side-by-side rulers.

```tsx
{
  decorators: [Story => <Box sx={{
    p: 3,
    height: 'calc(100vh - 32px)',
    bgcolor: 'grey.50',
    overflow: 'hidden'
  }}>
        <Story />
      </Box>],
  render: () => {
    const numericTags = [{
      id: 'yn1',
      name: 'Temperature (C)',
      color: '#1976d2',
      min: 0,
      max: 100
    }, {
      id: 'yn2',
      name: 'Pressure (bar)',
      color: '#ed6c02',
      min: 0,
      max: 100
    }, {
      id: 'yn3',
      name: 'Flow Rate (m3/h)',
      color: '#2e7d32',
      min: 25,
      max: 120
    }];
    const stringTags = [{
      id: 'ys1',
      name: 'Machine Status',
      color: '#0288d1'
    }, {
      id: 'ys2',
      name: 'Quality Grade',
      color: '#7b1fa2'
    }, {
      id: 'ys3',
      name: 'Operator Shift',
      color: '#c62828'
    }];
    const TagStringsWrapper = () => {
      const {
        surfaceRef,
        themeRef
      } = useChartTheme();
      const [optionsValues, setOptionsValues] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
        BandedAxis: false,
        MergeSimilarAxes: false
      });
      const initChart = useCallback(async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
        legendContainerId
      }: TrendChartInitOptions) => {
        const {
          sciChartSurface,
          wasmContext
        } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
          theme: themeRef.current
        });
        surfaceRef.current = sciChartSurface;
        sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildTimeXAxis(wasmContext, START, NOW));

        // Numeric axes
        numericTags.forEach((tag, i) => {
          const yAxis = buildYAxis(wasmContext, tag.id, tag.name, tag.min, tag.max, tag.color);
          yAxis.drawMajorGridLines = i === 0;
          sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(yAxis);
          const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
          const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
          const data = generateSineWave(START, 200, HOUR, amplitude, offset, amplitude * 0.3);
          sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, `sn${i}`, tag.name, data, tag.color, tag.id, false, {
            engLow: tag.min,
            engHigh: tag.max
          }));
        });

        // Modifiers first so the LegendModifier exists before string axes are added
        const stringAxisIds = new Set<string>();
        addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
          legendContainerId,
          stringAxisIds
        });

        // String axes (added after modifiers so they can be excluded from legend)
        stringTags.forEach(tag => {
          stringAxisIds.add(addStringAxis(sciChartSurface, wasmContext, tag));
          addStringSegments(sciChartSurface, tag.id, generateStringSegments(tag.name, START, NOW), tag.color);
        });
        return {
          sciChartSurface
        };
      }, [surfaceRef, themeRef]);
      const handleToggleOption = useCallback((key: string, next: boolean) => {
        const surface = surfaceRef.current;
        if (!surface || surface.isDeleted) return;
        setOptionsValues(prev => {
          const updated = {
            ...prev,
            [key]: next
          };
          if (key === 'MergeSimilarAxes') {
            setMergeSimilarAxes(surface, next);
            if (updated.BandedAxis) setBandedAxis(surface, true);
          }
          if (key === 'BandedAxis') {
            setBandedAxis(surface, next);
          }
          return updated;
        });
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const handleDefaultZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) setDefaultZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const handleAutoZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) doAutoZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      return <ChartCard>
          <ChartCard.Header>
            <ChartCard.Header.OptionsPanel items={[{
            key: 'BandedAxis',
            label: 'Banded Axis'
          }, {
            key: 'MergeSimilarAxes',
            label: 'Merge Similar Axes'
          }]} values={optionsValues} onToggle={handleToggleOption} />
            <ChartCard.Header.ChartControllers actionProps={{
            autoZoom: {
              onClick: handleAutoZoom
            },
            defaultZoom: {
              onClick: handleDefaultZoom
            }
          }} />
          </ChartCard.Header>
          <ChartCard.Body sx={{
          bgcolor: 'var(--background-paper-0, #f8f9fa)',
          flexDirection: 'column'
        }}>
            <TrendChart initChart={initChart} showLegend />
          </ChartCard.Body>
        </ChartCard>;
    };
    return <TagStringsWrapper />;
  }
}
```

### Banded Axis Multiple Tags

Banded Axis with 8 tags — each Y-axis occupies its own horizontal band.
Includes an OptionsPanel with a Banded Axis toggle to enable/disable.

```tsx
{
  decorators: [Story => <Box sx={{
    p: 3,
    height: 'calc(100vh - 32px)',
    bgcolor: 'grey.50',
    overflow: 'hidden'
  }}>
        <Story />
      </Box>],
  render: () => {
    const tags: {
      id: string;
      name: string;
      color: string;
      min: number;
      max: number;
    }[] = [{
      id: 'y1',
      name: 'Temperature (°C)',
      color: '#1976d2',
      min: 0,
      max: 100
    }, {
      id: 'y2',
      name: 'Pressure (bar)',
      color: '#ed6c02',
      min: 50,
      max: 100
    }, {
      id: 'y3',
      name: 'Flow Rate (m³/h)',
      color: '#2e7d32',
      min: 25,
      max: 120
    }, {
      id: 'y4',
      name: 'Viscosity (cP)',
      color: '#9c27b0',
      min: 50,
      max: 200
    }, {
      id: 'y5',
      name: 'pH Level',
      color: '#d32f2f',
      min: 0,
      max: 14
    }, {
      id: 'y6',
      name: 'Conductivity (µS)',
      color: '#0288d1',
      min: 100,
      max: 500
    }, {
      id: 'y7',
      name: 'Turbidity (NTU)',
      color: '#c2185b',
      min: 0,
      max: 50
    }, {
      id: 'y8',
      name: 'Dissolved O₂ (mg/L)',
      color: '#558b2f',
      min: 0,
      max: 15
    }];
    const Wrapper = () => {
      const {
        surfaceRef,
        themeRef
      } = useChartTheme();
      const [optionsValues, setOptionsValues] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
        BandedAxis: true
      });
      const initChart = useCallback(async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
        legendContainerId
      }: TrendChartInitOptions) => {
        const {
          sciChartSurface,
          wasmContext
        } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
          theme: themeRef.current
        });
        surfaceRef.current = sciChartSurface;
        sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildTimeXAxis(wasmContext, START, NOW));
        tags.forEach((tag, i) => {
          const yAxis = buildYAxis(wasmContext, tag.id, tag.name, tag.min, tag.max, tag.color);
          yAxis.drawMajorGridLines = true;
          sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(yAxis);
          const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
          const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
          const data = generateSineWave(START, 200, HOUR, amplitude, offset, amplitude * 0.2 + i * 0.5);
          sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, `s${i}`, tag.name, data, tag.color, tag.id, false, {
            engLow: tag.min,
            engHigh: tag.max
          }));
        });
        addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
          legendContainerId
        });
        setBandedAxis(sciChartSurface, true);
        return {
          sciChartSurface
        };
      }, [surfaceRef, themeRef]);
      const handleDefaultZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) setDefaultZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const handleAutoZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) doAutoZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      return <ChartCard>
          <ChartCard.Header>
            <ChartCard.Header.OptionsPanel items={[{
            key: 'BandedAxis',
            label: 'Banded Axis'
          }]} values={optionsValues} onToggle={(key: string, next: boolean) => {
            setOptionsValues((prev: Record<string, boolean>) => ({
              ...prev,
              [key]: next
            }));
            if (key === 'BandedAxis' && surfaceRef.current) {
              setBandedAxis(surfaceRef.current, next);
            }
          }} />
            <ChartCard.Header.ChartControllers actionProps={{
            autoZoom: {
              onClick: handleAutoZoom
            },
            defaultZoom: {
              onClick: handleDefaultZoom
            }
          }} />
          </ChartCard.Header>
          <ChartCard.Body sx={{
          bgcolor: 'var(--background-paper-0, #f8f9fa)',
          flexDirection: 'column'
        }}>
            <TrendChart initChart={initChart} showLegend={false} />
          </ChartCard.Body>
        </ChartCard>;
    };
    return <Wrapper />;
  }
}
```

### Tooltip Playground

**TooltipPlayground** — showcases every tooltip configuration.

Hover over each series to see different tooltip layouts:
- Full tooltip (unit, min/max limits, description, name)
- Partial tooltips (some fields missing)
- Hidden name / hidden description via `TooltipDisplayOptions`
- String tag tooltips with themed SVG styling

A reference card on the right lists what each series demonstrates.

```tsx
{
  decorators: [Story => <Box sx={{
    p: 2,
    height: 'calc(100vh - 32px)',
    bgcolor: 'grey.50',
    overflow: 'hidden',
    display: 'flex',
    flexDirection: 'column'
  }}>
        <Story />
      </Box>],
  render: () => {
    const TooltipPlaygroundWrapper = () => {
      const {
        surfaceRef,
        themeRef
      } = useChartTheme();
      const initChart = useCallback(async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
        legendContainerId
      }: TrendChartInitOptions) => {
        const {
          sciChartSurface,
          wasmContext
        } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
          theme: themeRef.current
        });
        surfaceRef.current = sciChartSurface;
        sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildTimeXAxis(wasmContext, START, NOW));

        // Numeric series — each with a different tooltip configuration
        TOOLTIP_SHOWCASE_TAGS.forEach((tag, i) => {
          const yAxis = buildYAxis(wasmContext, tag.id, tag.name, tag.min, tag.max, tag.color);
          yAxis.drawMajorGridLines = i === 0;
          sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(yAxis);
          const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
          const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
          const data = generateSineWave(START, 200, HOUR, amplitude, offset, amplitude * 0.3 + i * 0.7);
          sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, `s-${tag.id}`, tag.name, data, tag.color, tag.id, false, {
            engUnit: 'engUnit' in tag ? tag.engUnit : undefined,
            engLow: 'engLow' in tag ? tag.engLow : undefined,
            engHigh: 'engHigh' in tag ? tag.engHigh : undefined,
            description: 'description' in tag ? tag.description : undefined,
            displayOptions: {
              ...('showName' in tag ? {
                showName: tag.showName
              } : {}),
              ...('showDescription' in tag ? {
                showDescription: tag.showDescription
              } : {})
            }
          }));
        });

        // Modifiers + string axes
        const stringAxisIds = new Set<string>();
        addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
          legendContainerId,
          stringAxisIds
        });
        TOOLTIP_STRING_TAGS.forEach(tag => {
          stringAxisIds.add(addStringAxis(sciChartSurface, wasmContext, tag));
          addStringSegments(sciChartSurface, tag.id, generateStringSegments(tag.name, START, NOW), tag.color);
        });

          sciChartSurface
        };
      }, [surfaceRef, themeRef]);
      return <Box sx={{
        display: 'flex',
        height: '100%',

          flex: 1,
          minWidth: 0
        }}>
            <ChartCard>
              <ChartCard.Header>
                <ChartCard.Header.ChartControllers actionProps={{
                autoZoom: {
                  onClick: () => {
                    if (surfaceRef.current) doAutoZoom(surfaceRef.current);
                  }
                },
                defaultZoom: {
                  onClick: () => {
                    if (surfaceRef.current) setDefaultZoom(surfaceRef.current);
                  }
                }
              }} />
              </ChartCard.Header>
              <ChartCard.Body sx={{
              bgcolor: 'var(--background-paper-0, #f8f9fa)',
              flexDirection: 'column'
            }}>
                <TrendChart initChart={initChart} showLegend />
              </ChartCard.Body>
            </ChartCard>
          </Box>

          {/* Reference card */}
          <Box sx={{
          width: 320,
          flexShrink: 0,
          bgcolor: 'background.paper',
          borderRadius: 1,
          border: '1px solid',
          borderColor: 'divider',
          overflow: 'auto',
          p: 2
        }}>
            <Box sx={{
            fontSize: 14,
            fontWeight: 600,
            mb: 1.5
          }}>Tooltip Configurations</Box>
            <Box sx={{
            fontSize: 12,
            color: 'text.secondary',
            mb: 2
          }}>
              Hover over each series to see the tooltip. Each line exercises a different combination of fields.
            </Box>

            {TOOLTIP_SHOWCASE_TAGS.map(tag => <Box key={tag.id} sx={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'flex-start',
            gap: 1,
            mb: 1.5

              }}>{tag.name}</Box>
                  <Box sx={{
                color: 'text.secondary'
              }}>{tag.label}</Box>
                  <Box sx={{
                color: 'text.disabled',
                fontFamily: 'monospace',
                fontSize: 11,
                mt: 0.25
              }}>
                    {['engUnit' in tag ? `unit="${tag.engUnit}"` : null, 'engLow' in tag ? `min=${tag.engLow}` : null, 'engHigh' in tag ? `max=${tag.engHigh}` : null, 'description' in tag ? 'description=✓' : null, 'showName' in tag ? `showName=${tag.showName}` : null, 'showDescription' in tag ? `showDescription=${tag.showDescription}` : null].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')}
                  </Box>
                </Box>
              </Box>)}

            <Box sx={{
            borderTop: '1px solid',
            borderColor: 'divider',

              <Box sx={{
              fontSize: 11,

                Themed SVG tooltip with rounded corners, Gilroy font, CSS variables for light/dark support.
              </Box>
            </Box>
          </Box>
        </Box>;
    };
    return <TooltipPlaygroundWrapper />;
  }
```

### Default

Default: two series, two Y-axes, legend enabled, full modifiers.

```tsx
{
  decorators: boundedStoryDecorator,
  render: () => {
    const kpiData = generateSineWave(START, 200, HOUR, 30, 55, 5);
    const varData = generateRandomWalk(START, 200, HOUR, 15, 1.2);
    const buildChart = async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
      legendContainerId,
      theme,
      registerSurface
    }: StoryChartContext) => {
      const {
        sciChartSurface,
        wasmContext
      } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
        theme
      });
      registerSurface(sciChartSurface);
      sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildTimeXAxis(wasmContext, START, NOW));
      sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(buildYAxis(wasmContext, 'y1', 'Production KPI', 0, 100, '#1976d2'));
      sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(buildYAxis(wasmContext, 'y2', 'Variable', 0, 30, '#ed6c02'));
      sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, 's1', 'Production KPI', kpiData, '#1976d2', 'y1'));
      sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, 's2', 'Variable', varData, '#ed6c02', 'y2'));
      addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
        legendContainerId
      });
      return {
        sciChartSurface
      };
    };
    return <SimpleStoryChart buildChart={buildChart} />;
  }
}
```

### Merge Similar Axes

Merge Similar Axes — axes with the same range are merged into one.

7 tags with overlapping ranges:
- a, b: 0–100 → merged into 1 axis
- c, d: 0–50  → merged into 1 axis
- e, f: 50–100 → merged into 1 axis
- g: 60–80   → unique, stays alone

Both **Banded Axis** and **Merge Similar Axes** toggles are available.
All 4 combinations work:
- banded ✗ merge ✗ → 7 axes side-by-side (standard)
- banded ✓ merge ✗ → 7 axes stacked vertically
- banded ✗ merge ✓ → 4 axes side-by-side (merged)
- banded ✓ merge ✓ → 4 axes stacked vertically (merged)

```tsx
{
  decorators: [Story => <Box sx={{
    p: 3,
    height: 'calc(100vh - 32px)',
    bgcolor: 'grey.50',
    overflow: 'hidden'
  }}>
        <Story />
      </Box>],
  render: () => {
    const tags: {
      id: string;
      name: string;
      color: string;
      min: number;
      max: number;
    }[] = [{
      id: 'ya',
      name: 'Sensor A',
      color: '#1976d2',
      min: 0,
      max: 100
    }, {
      id: 'yb',
      name: 'Sensor B',
      color: '#0288d1',
      min: 0,
      max: 100
    }, {
      id: 'yc',
      name: 'Sensor C',
      color: '#2e7d32',
      min: 0,
      max: 50
    }, {
      id: 'yd',
      name: 'Sensor D',
      color: '#558b2f',
      min: 0,
      max: 50
    }, {
      id: 'ye',
      name: 'Sensor E',
      color: '#ed6c02',
      min: 50,
      max: 100
    }, {
      id: 'yf',
      name: 'Sensor F',
      color: '#c2185b',
      min: 50,
      max: 100
    }, {
      id: 'yg',
      name: 'Sensor G',
      color: '#9c27b0',
      min: 60,
      max: 80
    }];
    const Wrapper = () => {
      const {
        surfaceRef,
        themeRef
      } = useChartTheme();
      const [optionsValues, setOptionsValues] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
        BandedAxis: true,
        MergeSimilarAxes: true
      });

      /**
       * Apply merge + banded in the correct order.
       * Always: merge first (controls visibility) → banded second (calculates layout from visible axes).
       */
      const applyState = useCallback((banded: boolean, merge: boolean) => {
        const surface = surfaceRef.current;
        if (!surface) return;
        setMergeSimilarAxes(surface, merge);
        setBandedAxis(surface, banded);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const initChart = useCallback(async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
        legendContainerId
      }: TrendChartInitOptions) => {
        const {
          sciChartSurface,
          wasmContext
        } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
          theme: themeRef.current
        });
        surfaceRef.current = sciChartSurface;
        sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildTimeXAxis(wasmContext, START, NOW));
        tags.forEach((tag, i) => {
          const yAxis = buildYAxis(wasmContext, tag.id, tag.name, tag.min, tag.max, tag.color);
          yAxis.drawMajorGridLines = true;
          sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(yAxis);
          const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
          const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
          const data = generateSineWave(START, 200, HOUR, amplitude, offset, amplitude * 0.15 + i * 0.7);
          sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, `s${i}`, tag.name, data, tag.color, tag.id, false, {
            engLow: tag.min,
            engHigh: tag.max
          }));
        });
        addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
          legendContainerId
        });

        // Apply initial state (snapshot is created automatically by addDefaultModifiers)
        setMergeSimilarAxes(sciChartSurface, true);
        setBandedAxis(sciChartSurface, true);
        return {
          sciChartSurface
        };
      }, [surfaceRef, themeRef]);
      const handleDefaultZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) setDefaultZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      const handleAutoZoom = useCallback(() => {
        if (surfaceRef.current) doAutoZoom(surfaceRef.current);
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      return <ChartCard>
          <ChartCard.Header>
            <ChartCard.Header.OptionsPanel items={[{
            key: 'BandedAxis',
            label: 'Banded Axis'
          }, {
            key: 'MergeSimilarAxes',
            label: 'Merge Similar Axes'
          }]} values={optionsValues} onToggle={(key: string, next: boolean) => {
            setOptionsValues((prev: Record<string, boolean>) => {
              const updated = {
                ...prev,
                [key]: next
              };
              applyState(updated.BandedAxis, updated.MergeSimilarAxes);
              return updated;
            });
          }} />
            <ChartCard.Header.ChartControllers actionProps={{
            autoZoom: {
              onClick: handleAutoZoom
            },
            defaultZoom: {
              onClick: handleDefaultZoom
            }
          }} />
          </ChartCard.Header>
          <ChartCard.Body sx={{
          bgcolor: 'var(--background-paper-0, #f8f9fa)',
          flexDirection: 'column'
        }}>
            <TrendChart initChart={initChart} showLegend={false} />
          </ChartCard.Body>
        </ChartCard>;
    };
    return <Wrapper />;
  }
}
```

### Range Selection

**Range Selection** — drag to select an X range.

The range-selection modifier is generic: the selected `{ min, max }` can drive
statistics, a custom calculation, an export, or any data fetch for the window.
This story wires it to a **Statistics** toggle purely as one example consumer.

Flip the toggle on, then click-drag across the chart: a translucent band
follows the cursor over the full chart height. On mouse-up the selected
`{ min, max }` (X-axis data values — ms for a datetime axis) is reported.

While enabled, the left-button pan / axis-drag modifiers are suspended so the
selection owns the left mouse button; turning it off restores them and clears
the band. (RubberBand zoom stays available on the right mouse button.)

### Consuming it

```tsx
// Once, after addDefaultModifiers:
enableRangeSelection(surface, {
  // Fired on mouse-up with the final selection.
  onRangeSelected: async ({ min, max }) => {
    const stats = await api.fetchStats({ from: min, to: max });
    // … or any other use of the selected window …
  },
  // Optional live read-out while dragging.
  onRangeChange: ({ min, max }) => setLiveRange({ min, max }),
});

// Toggle from a UI switch. Enabling suspends the left-button pan/zoom drag;
// disabling restores it and clears the selection band.
setRangeSelectionEnabled(surface, enabled);

// Or read the last completed selection on demand:
const range = getSelectedRange(surface); // { min, max } | null
```

```tsx
{
  name: 'Range Selection',
  decorators: boundedStoryDecorator,
  render: () => {
    const Wrapper = () => {
      const {
        surfaceRef,
        themeRef
      } = useChartTheme();
      const [statsRange, setStatsRange] = useState<SelectedRange | null>(null);
      const [optionsValues, setOptionsValues] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
        Statistics: true
      });
      const initChart = useCallback(async (root: string | HTMLDivElement, {
        legendContainerId
      }: TrendChartInitOptions) => {
        const {
          sciChartSurface,
          wasmContext
        } = await SciChartSurface.create(root, {
          theme: themeRef.current
        });
        surfaceRef.current = sciChartSurface;
        sciChartSurface.xAxes.add(buildTimeXAxis(wasmContext, START, NOW));
        UON_NUMERIC_TAGS.forEach((tag, i) => {
          const yAxis = buildYAxis(wasmContext, tag.id, tag.name, tag.min, tag.max, tag.color);
          yAxis.drawMajorGridLines = i === 0;
          sciChartSurface.yAxes.add(yAxis);
          const amplitude = (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.3;
          const offset = tag.min + (tag.max - tag.min) * 0.5;
          const data = mockTrendSeries(START, NOW, amplitude, offset, i * 0.8);
          sciChartSurface.renderableSeries.add(buildSeries(wasmContext, `sn${i}`, tag.name, data, tag.color, tag.id, false, {
            engLow: tag.min,
            engHigh: tag.max
          }));
        });
        addDefaultModifiers(sciChartSurface, {
          legendContainerId
        });

        // Register the range-selection modifier and start it enabled to match
        // the toggle's default.
        enableRangeSelection(sciChartSurface, {
          onRangeChange: range => setStatsRange(range),
          onRangeSelected: range => setStatsRange(range)
        });
        setRangeSelectionEnabled(sciChartSurface, true);
        return {
          sciChartSurface
        };
      }, [surfaceRef, themeRef]);
      const handleToggleOption = useCallback((key: string, next: boolean) => {
        const surface = surfaceRef.current;
        if (!surface || surface.isDeleted) return;
        setOptionsValues(prev => ({
          ...prev,
          [key]: next
        }));
        if (key === 'Statistics') {
          setRangeSelectionEnabled(surface, next);
          if (!next) setStatsRange(null);
        }
      }, [surfaceRef]);
      return <Box sx={{
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        height: '100%',
        gap: 1
      }}>
          <Box sx={{
          flex: 1,
          minHeight: 0
        }}>
            <ChartCard>
              <ChartCard.Header>
                <ChartCard.Header.OptionsPanel items={[{
                key: 'Statistics',
                label: 'Statistics'
              }]} values={optionsValues} onToggle={handleToggleOption} />
                <ChartCard.Header.ChartControllers />
              </ChartCard.Header>
              <ChartCard.Body sx={{
              bgcolor: 'var(--background-paper-0, #f8f9fa)',
              flexDirection: 'column'
            }}>
                <TrendChart initChart={initChart} showLegend />
              </ChartCard.Body>
            </ChartCard>
          </Box>
          <Box sx={{
          fontSize: 12,
          color: 'text.secondary',
          px: 1
        }}>
            {optionsValues.Statistics ? statsRange ? <Box component="span" sx={{
            fontWeight: 600,
            color: 'primary.main'
          }}>
                  Selected range: {new Date(statsRange.min).toLocaleString()} →{' '}
                  {new Date(statsRange.max).toLocaleString()}
                </Box> : 'Drag on the chart to select a range' : 'Statistics off — toggle it on to select a range'}
          </Box>
        </Box>;
    };
    return <Wrapper />;
  }
}
```

### Predefined Initial State

**PredefinedInitialState** — a configurator screen where you pick the initial
state (merge, banded, graphic/legend options, zoom/pan/rollover/mouse-wheel),
then click **Initialize the trend** to boot it with exactly those values via a
single `applyTrendInitialState(surface, config)` call. Use **Reconfigure** to
go back and try another combination.

```tsx
{
  decorators: boundedStoryDecorator,
  render: () => <PredefinedInitialStateWrapper />
}
```
</file>

<file path="design-system-data-display-avatar.txt">
# Design System/data-display/Avatar

Avatars are found throughout material design with uses in everything from tables to dialog menus.

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `variant` | `"circular"` | No | circular | The shape of the avatar. Only `'circular'` is supported in the Metris design system. |
| `size` | `"xs" "sm" "md" "lg"` | No | lg | Controls the rendered dimensions of the avatar. `'xs'` = 18 px, `'sm'` = 24 px, `'md'` = 32 px, `'lg'` = 40 px. |
| `ref` | `((instance: HTMLDivElement | null) => void) | RefObject<HTMLDivElement> | null` | No |  |  |
| `slots` | `Partial<AvatarSlots>` | No | {} | The components used for each slot inside. |
| `slotProps` | `{ root?: SlotProps<"div", AvatarRootSlotPropsOverrides, AvatarOwnProps>; img?: SlotProps<"img", AvatarImgSlotPropsOverrides, AvatarOwnProps>; fallback?: SlotProps<...> | undefined; } | undefined` | No | {} | The props used for each slot inside. |
| `component` | `ElementType<any, keyof IntrinsicElements>` | No |  |  |


## Examples


### Avatar Group With Sizes

```tsx

    <AvatarGroup max={2}>
```

<file path="design-system-data-display-badge.txt">
# Design System/data-display/Badge

Badge generates a small badge to the top-right of its child(ren).

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `color` | `"error" "success" "warning" "primary"` | No | primary | Drives the badge's background color using the Metris palette. |
| `ref` | `((instance: HTMLSpanElement | null) => void) | RefObject<HTMLSpanElement> | null` | No |  |  |
| `slots` | `Partial<BadgeSlots>` | No | {} | The components used for each slot inside. |
| `slotProps` | `{ root?: SlotProps<"span", BadgeRootSlotPropsOverrides, { children?: ReactNode; className?: string; anchorOrigin: BadgeOrigin; ... 11 more ...; displayValue: ReactNode; }>; badge?: SlotProps<...> | undefined; } | undefined` | No | {} | The props used for each slot inside. |
| `component` | `ElementType<any, keyof IntrinsicElements>` | No |  |  |


## Examples


### With Avatar

```tsx
args => <Stack direction="row" spacing={2} sx={{
  m: 4
}}>
    <Badge {...args} badgeContent={5} sx={{
    width: 36,
    height: 36
  }}>
      <Avatar alt="User Avatar" src="https://mui.com/static/images/avatar/1.jpg" />
    </Badge>
    <Badge {...args} badgeContent={10} sx={{
    width: 36,
    height: 36
  }}>
      <Avatar alt="User Avatar" src="https://mui.com/static/images/avatar/2.jpg" />
    </Badge>
  </Stack>
```

### With Numbers

```tsx
args => <Stack direction="row" spacing={3} sx={{
  m: 4
}}>
    <Stack alignItems="center" spacing={1}>
      <Badge {...args} badgeContent={5} sx={{
      height: 36,
      width: 36
    }}>
        <MailIcon sx={{
        height: 20,
        width: 20
      }} />
      </Badge>
      <Typography variant="caption">Single Digit</Typography>
    </Stack>

    <Stack alignItems="center" spacing={1}>
      <Badge {...args} badgeContent={25} sx={{
      height: 36,
      width: 36
    }}>
        <NotificationsIcon sx={{
        height: 20,
        width: 20
      }} />
      </Badge>
      <Typography variant="caption">Double Digit</Typography>
    </Stack>

    <Stack alignItems="center" spacing={1}>
      <Badge {...args} badgeContent={100} sx={{
      height: 36,
      width: 36
    }}>
        <ShoppingCartIcon sx={{
        height: 20,
        width: 20
      }} />
      </Badge>
      <Typography variant="caption">Default Max (99+)</Typography>
    </Stack>

    <Stack alignItems="center" spacing={1}>
      <Badge {...args} badgeContent={999} max={999} sx={{
      height: 36,
      width: 36
    }}>
        <IconButton>
          <RocketLaunchOutlinedIcon sx={{
          height: 20,
          width: 20
        }} />
        </IconButton>
      </Badge>
      <Typography variant="caption">Custom Max</Typography>
    </Stack>
  </Stack>
```

### Badge Positioning

Badge Positioning

Demonstrates different anchor positions for badges to show flexibility in placement.

```tsx
() => <Stack spacing={6}>
    <Typography variant="h4">Badge Positioning Options</Typography>

    <Grid container spacing={4}>
      {[{
      vertical: 'top',
      horizontal: 'right',
      label: 'Top Right (Default)'
    }, {
      vertical: 'top',
      horizontal: 'left',
      label: 'Top Left'
    }, {
      vertical: 'bottom',
      horizontal: 'right',
      label: 'Bottom Right'
    }, {
      vertical: 'bottom',
      horizontal: 'left',
      label: 'Bottom Left'
    }].map(position => <Grid key={position.label} sx={{
      width: {
        xs: '50%',
        sm: '25%'
      }
    }}>
          <Stack alignItems="center" spacing={1}>
            <Badge badgeContent={4} color="primary" anchorOrigin={{
          vertical: position.vertical as 'top' | 'bottom',
          horizontal: position.horizontal as 'left' | 'right'
        }} sx={{
          p: 2
        }}>
              <Box sx={{
            width: 80,
            height: 80,
            bgcolor: 'background.paper',
            border: '1px dashed grey.300',
            borderRadius: 1,
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center'
          }}>
                Box
              </Box>
            </Badge>
            <Typography variant="caption">{position.label}</Typography>
          </Stack>
        </Grid>)}
    </Grid>
  </Stack>
```

<file path="design-system-data-display-chip.txt">
# Design System/data-display/Chip

Chips are compact elements that represent an input, attribute, or action.

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `color` | `"default" "error" "success" "warning" "primary"` | No | default | Drives the chip's color tokens: border for `outlined` variant; hover/focus background for the `filled` variant. |
| `ref` | `((instance: HTMLDivElement | null) => void) | RefObject<HTMLDivElement> | null` | No |  |  |
| `slots` | `Partial<ChipSlots>` | No | {} | The components used for each slot inside. |
| `slotProps` | `{ root?: SlotProps<"div", {}, ChipOwnerState>; label?: SlotProps<"span", {}, ChipOwnerState>; } | undefined` | No | {} | The props used for each slot inside. |
| `component` | `ElementType<any, keyof IntrinsicElements>` | No |  |  |


## Examples


### With Icons And Avatars

Chips with Icons and Avatars

Shows different ways to combine chips with icons and avatars.

```tsx
{
  render: () => <Stack spacing={3}>
      <Typography variant="subtitle2">Chips with Icons</Typography>
      <Stack direction="row" spacing={1} useFlexGap flexWrap="wrap">
        <Chip icon={<FaceIcon />} label="With Icon" />
        <Chip icon={<FaceIcon />} label="Primary" color="primary" />
        <Chip icon={<FaceIcon />} label="Outlined" variant="outlined" />
      </Stack>

      <Typography variant="subtitle2">Chips with Avatars</Typography>
      <Stack direction="row" spacing={1} useFlexGap flexWrap="wrap">
        <Chip avatar={<Avatar>M</Avatar>} label="With Avatar" />
        <Chip avatar={<Avatar>A</Avatar>} label="Primary" color="primary" />
        <Chip avatar={<Avatar>B</Avatar>} label="Outlined" variant="outlined" />
      </Stack>

      <Typography variant="subtitle2">Combination with Delete</Typography>
      <Stack direction="row" spacing={1} useFlexGap flexWrap="wrap">
        <Chip avatar={<Avatar>M</Avatar>} label="Avatar + Delete" onDelete={() => {}} />
        <Chip icon={<FaceIcon />} label="Icon + Delete" onDelete={() => {}} color="primary" />

}
```

# Design System/data-display/Divider

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `sx` | `SxProps<Theme>` | No |  | The system prop that allows defining system overrides as well as additional CSS styles. |
| `ref` | `((instance: HTMLHRElement | null) => void) | RefObject<HTMLHRElement> | null` | No |  |  |
| `component` | `ElementType<any, keyof IntrinsicElements>` | No |  |  |


## Examples


### Horizontal

```tsx
{
  args: {
    orientation: 'horizontal',
    sx: {
      my: 2
    }
  },
  render: args => <Box>
      <Typography>Above</Typography>
      <Divider {...args} />
      <Typography>Below</Typography>
    </Box>
}
```

### Playground

```tsx
{
  args: {
    orientation: 'horizontal',
    variant: 'fullWidth',
    flexItem: false,
    textAlign: 'center'
  },
  render: args => <Box sx={{
    width: 300
  }}>
      <Typography>Above the divider</Typography>
      <Divider {...args} sx={{
      my: 2
    }} />
      <Typography>Below the divider</Typography>
    </Box>
}
```

### Styled

```tsx
{
  args: {
    sx: {
      borderColor: 'primary.main',
      borderWidth: 2,
      my: 3
    }
  },
  render: args => <Box>
      <Typography>Custom Styled Divider</Typography>
      <Divider {...args} />
    </Box>
}
```

### Vertical

```tsx
{
  args: {
    orientation: 'vertical',
    flexItem: true,
    sx: {
      mx: 2,
      height: 40
    }
  },
  render: args => <Box display="flex" alignItems="center">
      <Typography>Left</Typography>
      <Divider {...args} />
      <Typography>Right</Typography>
    </Box>
}
```

# Design System/data-display/ImageGallery

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `images` | `string[] | GalleryImage[]` | Yes |  | List of images to display. Each item is either a URL string or a `GalleryImage` object with an optional caption. |
| `initialImageIndex` | `number` | No | 1 | 1-based index of the image to show initially. |
| `loading` | `boolean` | Yes |  | When `true`, navigation controls are disabled and a loading skeleton is shown. |
| `width` | `string` | No |  | CSS width of each image (e.g. `'496px'`). Passed to the underlying viewer. |
| `height` | `string` | No |  | CSS height of each image (e.g. `'279px'`). Passed to the underlying viewer. |
| `autoCrop` | `"bottom" "left" "right" "top" "center" "top-left" "top-right" "bottom-left" "bottom-right"` | No | center | Auto-crop position. When set, images are sized to fill `width` × `height` with `object-fit: cover`. |


## Examples


### Loading

```tsx
{
  args: {
    images,
    initialImageIndex: 1,
    loading: true,
    width: '496px',
    height: '279px',
    autoCrop: 'center'
  }
}
```

### Empty

```tsx
{
  args: {
    images: [],
    initialImageIndex: 1,
    loading: false,
    width: '496px',
    height: '279px',
    autoCrop: 'center'
  }
}
```

```tsx
{
  args: {
    images,
    initialImageIndex: 1,
    loading: false,
    width: '496px',
    height: '279px',
    autoCrop: 'center'
  }
}
```

<file path="design-system-data-display-imageviewer.txt">
# Design System/data-display/ImageViewer

An image viewer component with preview functionality, zoom, rotation, and download capabilities.

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `alt` | `string` | No |  | Alternate text for the image, displayed when the image cannot be rendered. |
| `autoCrop` | `"bottom" "left" "right" "top" "center" "top-left" "top-right" "bottom-left" "bottom-right"` | No |  | Crop position when auto-filling the container. When set, the image is sized to `width`/`height` with `object-fit: cover`. |
| `downloadable` | `boolean` | No | false | Show a download button inside the preview toolbar. |
| `downloadIcon` | `ReactNode` | No |  | Custom icon rendered on the download button in the preview toolbar. |
| `height` | `string` | No |  | CSS height of the image element (e.g. `'200px'`). |
| `imageClassName` | `string` | No |  | Additional CSS class applied directly to the `<img>` element. |
| `imageStyle` | `CSSProperties` | No |  | Inline styles applied directly to the `<img>` element. |
| `indicatorIcon` | `ReactNode` | No |  | Custom icon shown in the hover overlay when `preview` is enabled. Defaults to a visibility eye icon. |
| `loading` | `"eager" "lazy"` | No | lazy | Browser loading strategy for the image. |
| `preview` | `boolean` | No | false | Enable preview mode — clicking the image opens a full-screen overlay with zoom and rotation controls. |
| `referrerPolicy` | `"" "origin" "no-referrer" "no-referrer-when-downgrade" "origin-when-cross-origin" "same-origin" "strict-origin" "strict-origin-when-cross-origin" "unsafe-url"` | No |  | Controls how much referrer information is sent with the image request. |
| `src` | `string` | Yes |  | URL of the image to display. |
| `width` | `string` | No |  | CSS width of the image element (e.g. `'300px'`). |
| `zoomSrc` | `string` | No |  | High-resolution image URL used in the preview overlay zoom view. Falls back to `src` if omitted. |
| `onHide` | `(() => void)` | No |  | Callback fired when the preview overlay is closed. |
| `onShow` | `(() => void)` | No |  | Callback fired when the preview overlay is opened. |
| `onDownload` | `(() => void)` | No |  | Callback fired when the user triggers a download from the preview toolbar. |
| `className` | `string` | No |  | Additional CSS class applied to the root container `<div>`. |
| `style` | `CSSProperties` | No |  | Inline styles applied to the root container `<div>`. |


## Examples


### Different Sizes

```tsx
{
  render: () => <Box sx={{
    display: 'flex',
    gap: 2,
    flexWrap: 'wrap',
    alignItems: 'center'
  }}>
      <ImageViewer src={sampleImage} alt="Small image" width="80px" height="60px" preview />
      <ImageViewer src={sampleImage} alt="Medium image" width="150px" height="100px" preview />
      <ImageViewer src={sampleImage} alt="Large image" width="250px" height="180px" preview />
    </Box>
}
```

### Eager Loading

```tsx
{
  args: {
    src: sampleImage,
    alt: 'Eager loaded image',
    width: '250px',
    loading: 'eager',
    preview: true
  }
}
```

### Lazy Loading

```tsx
{
  args: {
    src: sampleImage,
    alt: 'Lazy loaded image',
    width: '250px',
    loading: 'lazy',
    preview: true
  }
}
```

### Playground

```tsx
{
  args: {
    src: sampleImage,
    alt: 'Sample landscape image',
    width: '250px',
    height: undefined,
    preview: false,
    downloadable: false,
    loading: 'eager'
  }
}
```

### With Custom Download Handler

```tsx
    src: sampleImage,

    width: '250px',

  }
}
```

```tsx
      }

  }
```
```tsx
}

  }
```

<file path="design-system-data-display-list.txt">
# Design System/data-display/List

Lists are continuous, vertical indexes of text or images. Lists are a continuous group of text or images. They are composed of items containing primary and supplemental actions, which are represented by icons and text.

## Props


| Name | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|------|------|----------|---------|-------------|
| `component` | `ElementType<any, keyof IntrinsicElements>` | No |  |  |


## Examples


### Secondary Actions

List with secondary actions.
This story demonstrates list items with primary content and secondary actions
such as delete, favorite, or more options.

```tsx
{
  render: args => <Box sx={{
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 360,
    bgcolor: 'background.paper'
  }}>
      <List {...args}>
        <ListSubheader>Recent Conversations</ListSubheader>
        {[{
        id: 'conv1',
        name: 'Sarah Johnson',
        message: 'Can you review the document?',
        time: '5 mins ago'
      }, {
        id: 'conv2',
        name: 'David Lee',
        message: 'Meeting scheduled',
        time: '1 hour ago'
      }, {
        id: 'conv3',
        name: 'Emma Wilson',
        message: 'Thanks for your help!',
        time: '3 hours ago'
      }].map(item => <ListItem key={item.id} secondaryAction={<IconButton edge="end" aria-label="delete">
                <DeleteIcon fontSize="small" />
              </IconButton>}>
            <ListItemAvatar>
              <Avatar>{item.name.charAt(0)}</Avatar>
            </ListItemAvatar>
            <ListItemText primary={item.name} secondary={<>
                  <Typography component="span" variant="body2" color="text.primary" sx={{
            display: 'block'
          }}>
                    {item.message}
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography component="span" variant="caption" color="text.secondary">
                    {item.time}
                  </Typography>
                </>} />
          </ListItem>)}
      </List>
    </Box>
}
```

### List Item States

Various list item states in one view.
This story demonstrates different states including selected, disabled, and focused items.

```tsx
{
  render: args => <Box sx={{
    width: '100%',
    maxWidth: 360,
    bgcolor: 'background.paper'
  }}>
      <List {...args} subheader={<ListSubheader>Item States</ListSubheader>}>
        <ListItem>
          <ListItemButton>
            <ListItemText primary="Default state" secondary="Regular list item" />
          </ListItemButton>
        </ListItem>

        <ListItem>
          <ListItemButton selected>
            <ListItemText primary="Selected state" secondary="Currently active item" />
          </ListItemButton>
        </ListItem>

        <ListItem>
          <ListItemButton disabled>
            <ListItemText primary="Disabled state" secondary="Unavailable for interaction" />
          </ListItemButton>
        </ListItem>

        <ListItem divider>
          <ListItemButton>
            <ListItemText primary="With divider" secondary="Has a bottom divider" />
          </ListItemButton>
        </ListItem>

        <ListItem>
          <ListItemButton dense>
            <ListItemText primary="Dense list item" secondary="Compact version with less vertical padding" slotProps={{
            secondary: {
              noWrap: true
            }
          }} />
          </ListItemButton>
        </ListItem>
      </List>
    </Box>
}
```

### Nested List

Nested list with expandable sections.
This story demonstrates how to create hierarchical, collapsible list sections.

```tsx
{
  render: function NestedListRender(args) {
    const [open, setOpen] = useState({
      inbox: true,
      drafts: false,
      sent: false
    });
    const handleClick = (section: keyof typeof open) => () => {
      setOpen({
        ...open,
        [section]: !open[section]
      });
    };
    return <Box sx={{
      width: '100%',
      maxWidth: 360,
      bgcolor: 'background.paper'
    }}>
        <List {...args} sx={{
        width: '100%',
        maxWidth: 360,
        bgcolor: 'background.paper'
      }} component="nav" aria-labelledby="nested-list-subheader" subheader={<ListSubheader component="div" id="nested-list-subheader">
              Email Folders
            </ListSubheader>}>
          <ListItemButton onClick={handleClick('inbox')}>
            <ListItemIcon>
              <InboxIcon />
            </ListItemIcon>
            <ListItemText primary="Inbox" secondary="12 new messages" />
            {open.inbox ? <ExpandLess /> : <ExpandMore />}
          </ListItemButton>
          <List component="div" disablePadding sx={{
          display: open.inbox ? 'block' : 'none'
        }}>
            <ListItemButton sx={{
            pl: 4
          }}>
              <ListItemIcon>
                <StarBorder />
              </ListItemIcon>
              <ListItemText primary="Important" />
            </ListItemButton>
            <ListItemButton sx={{
            pl: 4
          }}>
              <ListItemIcon>
                <LabelIcon />
              </ListItemIcon>
              <ListItemText primary="Promotions" />
            </ListItemButton>
          </List>

          <ListItemButton onClick={handleClick('drafts')}>
            <ListItemIcon>
              <DraftsIcon />
            </ListItemIcon>
            <ListItemText primary="Drafts" secondary="3 drafts" />
            {open.drafts ? <ExpandLess /> : <ExpandMore />}
          </ListItemButton>
          <List component="div" disablePadding sx={{
          display: open.drafts ? 'block' : 'none'
        }}>
            <ListItemButton sx={{
            pl: 4
          }}>
              <ListItemText primary="Project proposal" />
            </ListItemButton>
            <ListItemButton sx={{
            pl: 4
          }}>
              <ListItemText primary="Weekly report" />
            </ListItemButton>
          </List>

          <ListItemButton onClick={handleClick('sent')}>
            <ListItemIcon>
              <SendIcon />
            </ListItemIcon>
            <ListItemText primary="Sent" />
            {open.sent ? <ExpandLess /> : <ExpandMore />}
          </ListItemButton>
          <List component="div" disablePadding sx={{
          display: open.sent ? 'block' : 'none'
        }}>
            <ListItemButton sx={{
            pl: 4
          }}>
              <ListItemText primary="Last 7 days" />
            </ListItemButton>
            <ListItemButton sx={{
            pl: 4
          }}>
              <ListItemText primary="Last 30 days" />
            </ListItemButton>
          </List>
        </List>
      </Box>;
  }
}
```

### Notification List

Notification list with different states.
This story demonstrates a notification center with read/unread states and actions.

```tsx
{
  render: function NotificationListRender(args) {
    const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState([{
      id: 1,
      content: 'Your project "Dashboard" was approved',
      time: '5 mins ago',
      read: false
    }, {
      id: 2,
      content: 'John commented on your document',
      time: '1 hour ago',
      read: false
    }, {
      id: 3,
      content: 'New team member Sarah has joined your project',
      time: '3 hours ago',
      read: true
    }, {
      id: 4,
      content: 'Reminder: Meeting starts in 30 minutes',
      time: 'Yesterday, 5:30 PM',
      read: true
    }]);
    const removeNotification = (id: number) => {
      setNotifications(prev => prev.filter(notification => notification.id !== id));
    };
    return <Box sx={{
      width: '100%',
      maxWidth: 360,
      bgcolor: 'background.paper'
    }}>
        <List {...args} subheader={<ListSubheader sx={{
        display: 'flex',
        justifyContent: 'space-between',
        alignItems: 'center',
        py: 4
      }}>
              <Typography variant="subtitle2">Notifications</Typography>
              <Typography variant="caption" color="primary" sx={{
          cursor: 'pointer'
        }} onClick={() => setNotifications(prev => prev.map(n => ({
          ...n,
          read: true
        })))}>
                Mark all as read
              </Typography>
            </ListSubheader>}>
          {notifications.length === 0 ? <ListItem>
              <ListItemText primary="No notifications" slotProps={{
            primary: {
              align: 'center',
              color: 'text.secondary'
            }
          }} />
            </ListItem> : notifications.map(notification => <ListItem key={notification.id} alignItems="flex-start" sx={{
          bgcolor: notification.read ? 'transparent' : 'action.hover',
          transition: 'background-color 0.2s ease',
          '&:hover': {
            bgcolor: 'action.selected'
          }
        }} secondaryAction={<IconButton edge="end" aria-label="delete" onClick={() => removeNotification(notification.id)}>
                    <DeleteIcon fontSize="small" />
                  </IconButton>}>
                <ListItemAvatar>
                  <Avatar sx={{
              bgcolor: notification.read ? 'grey.400' : 'primary.main'
            }}>
                    <NotificationsIcon />
                  </Avatar>
                </ListItemAvatar>
                <ListItemText primary={<Typography variant="body2" component="span" sx={{
            fontWeight: notification.read ? 'normal' : 'bold'
          }}>
                      {notification.content}
                    </Typography>} secondary={<Typography variant="caption" color="text.secondary" component="span">
                      {notification.time}
                    </Typography>} />
              </ListItem>)}
        </List>
      </Box>;
  }
}
```

### Selection Patterns

Interactive selection with ListItemButton.
This story demonstrates how to implement single and multiple selection patterns
in lists, with proper styling and state management.

```tsx
{
  render: function SelectionPatternsRender(args) {
    // State for single selection (radio-like behavior)
    const [selectedItem, setSelectedItem] = useState<string | null>('inbox');

    // Toggle single selection (only one item can be selected)
    const handleSingleSelect = (value: string) => {
      setSelectedItem(value);
    };
    const folders = ['inbox', 'drafts', 'sent', 'trash', 'spam'];
    const folderIconMap: Record<string, React.ComponentType> = {
      inbox: InboxIcon,
      drafts: DraftsIcon,
      sent: SendIcon,
      trash: DeleteIcon
    };
    return <Box sx={{
      width: '100%',
      maxWidth: 360,
      bgcolor: 'background.paper'
    }}>
        <List {...args} subheader={<ListSubheader sx={{
        display: 'flex',
        justifyContent: 'space-between'
      }}>
              <Typography variant="subtitle2">Single Selection</Typography>
              <Typography variant="caption" color="text.secondary">
                Selected: {selectedItem || 'none'}
              </Typography>
            </ListSubheader>}>
          {folders.map(folder => {
          const FolderItemIcon = folderIconMap[folder] ?? ReportIcon;
          return <ListItem key={folder} disablePadding>
                <ListItemButton selected={selectedItem === folder} onClick={() => handleSingleSelect(folder)}>
                  <ListItemIcon>
                    <FolderItemIcon />
                  </ListItemIcon>
                  <ListItemText primary={folder.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + folder.slice(1)} secondary={selectedItem === folder ? 'Currently selected' : 'Click to select'} />
                </ListItemButton>
              </ListItem>;
        })}
        </List>
      </Box>;
  }
}
```
